# SAME-NAME PERSON PAIRS - the backlog, and the baseline this gate fires against.
#
# tools/check-duplicates.py flags two register rows sharing a forename and a canonical
# surname AND something only the same person would share. Every pair it finds must be
# listed here, so a NEW one fails the build and gets looked at while it is still fresh.
#
# 133 PAIRS WERE PRESENT when this file was first written, on 20 September 2026.
# *** 123 ARE LISTED NOW, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM CARRIES A REASON. *** The backlog was worked to
# the end on the day it was opened: 123 compared, and the merges that came out of it took the
# register from 292 people to 286.
#
# *** A LINE WITHOUT A REASON IS A PAIR NOBODY HAS LOOKED AT. *** There are none today. When the
# gate adds one it will sit here bare, and that is the signal. Write the reason after a #:
#   Name A || Name B   # different men: one d.1878 Montevideo, one arr. Buenos Aires 1926
#
# The list exists because this archive added a duplicate of Dominga Josefa Llerena on the
# day this checker was written - one hour after refusing to add duplicates on principle.
# Every other gate here catches BROKEN data. Nothing caught REDUNDANT data, because a
# duplicate person is perfectly well-formed: it renders, it carries evidence, it passes.
Bernabe LERENA (of Trinidad) || Bernabe LERENA (1911 dispensation)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN on different wives and different towns. The Trinidad man's wife is CASIMIRA PELAEZ and his four children run 1871-1885; the dispensation man married FLORENCIA CARDOZO at Montevideo and fathered Custodio. And the Trinidad row itself argues the forename should repeat - six or seven men of this surname in one small town are 'almost certainly brothers or first cousins'. A second Bernabe is what that kindred predicts.
Emilia LERENA || Emilia LERENA Vega (b. c.1832)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN. One b.1886 in SPAIN, in Leandro's 1895 household; the other b.c.1815 in MONTEVIDEO, daughter of Pedro Antonio Lerena and Vicenta Vega. Seventy-one years and two countries apart.
Felisa LERENA (arr. 1911) || Felisa LERENA (arr. 1923)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: GENUINELY UNSETTLED, and left that way on purpose. b.1893 arriving with Gregorio, and b.1891 arriving with four children - a woman who sailed once and came back with a family is an ordinary thing, and two years of drift in a stated age is less than this archive sees weekly. Neither merged nor declared distinct: there is no evidence either way.
Fidel LERENA Fernandez || Fidel LERENA (m. Maria Ruedas)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, HELD APART ON PURPOSE. A rare forename twice in one city. Sancho's Fidel has a tree-only death in 1822 which, if wrong, makes the merge possible. TEST RUN 20 Sep THROUGH THE UI, BOTH HALVES NULL, AND IT WEAKENS THE SEPARATION RATHER THAN THE MERGE: no marriage of Fidel Lerena to Maria Rueda(s) is indexed (control: Luis x Juanico returns the 1864 act at rank one), and no burial of a Fidel Lerena 1815-1840 (control: Rodriguez in that window returns 62,026 with real burials, so the window is populated). The trees' 1822 death was the one thing holding these two apart and it is not in the record. Still not merged - an unevidenced death is not evidence of survival.
Francisco LERENA (b.1890, 1895 census) || Francisco LERENA (b.1891, arr. 1930)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED. The census boy is five in Leandro's Buenos Aires household in 1895, born Argentina; the traveller is born 1891 and lands in 1930, so an Argentine returning home. The years fit within one. Nothing else connects them, and Francisco is not a rare name.
Francisco LERENA (b.1890, 1895 census) || Francisco LLERENA (arr. 1923-25)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: SAME QUESTION AS THE LINE ABOVE, since these two arrivals are themselves a candidate pair. If the two travellers are one man, this is the same identification put twice.
Francisco LERENA (b.1891, arr. 1930) || Francisco LERENA (b.1923, arr. 1930)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON on one passenger list - the younger row reads 'Son of the above', thirty-two years apart, landed together 25 October 1930.
Francisco LERENA (b.1891, arr. 1930) || Francisco LLERENA (arr. 1923-25)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: STRONG CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED - and the corroboration is a CHILD rather than an age. Both men are born 1891 and both travel with an ARGENTINE-BORN SON. The 1925 crossing carries an infant; the 1930 crossing carries a Francisco aged seven, which is a birth about 1923. LLERENA is one of this family's seven spellings. Not merged because nothing but the passenger lists says so, and this archive joined two Arturo Lerenas on less. TEST: the CEMLA entries' stated ages and marital status, and whether the 1923 and 1930 lists give the same ship's origin.
Gilberto LERENA (Gilberto Justiniano LERENA LENGUAS) || Gilberto LERENA (b.1879, arr. 1928)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN whichever way the 1928 arrival resolves. The Stud Book Gilberto was 41 in 1895 and born 1854; the traveller was born 1879. If the traveller is the Salvanach son, these two are father and son.
Gilberto LERENA (Gilberto Justiniano LERENA LENGUAS) || Gilberto LERENA Salvanach (the younger, b.1878)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON. The Stud Book Gilberto, born 1854, and his eldest, born Montevideo 1878 - line 6 of the 1895 Buenos Aires schedule, 'Salvanach Lerena, GILBERTO, varon, 17, soltero'.
Gilberto LERENA (b.1879, arr. 1928) || Gilberto LERENA (b.1916, arr. 1928)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON on one passenger list, 37 years apart - comercio and estudiante, landed together 27 Feb 1928.
Gilberto LERENA (b.1879, arr. 1928) || Gilberto LERENA Salvanach (the younger, b.1878)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: STRONG CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED. The only stated objection - "not in the 1895 household" - was false and is retracted; he is line 6 of that schedule at 17. Birth years one apart, Argentine nationality, Buenos Aires, and a son Gilberto b.1916 travelling with him against a marriage of 1906. Both rows index-level. TEST PART-RUN 20 Sep: the military record gives birth 1878 and Distrito Militar 2, March 1922 - consistent, not decisive. The son is now documented (Gilberto de Jesus del Corazon, by Cipriana Saenz Pena, m. 19 May 1906) but carries no indexed birth year, so 1916 is unconfirmed. Remaining: the CEMLA marital status, or that child's birth record.
Gregorio LERENA (arr. 1911) || Gregorio LERENA (arr. 1924)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED - the 1911 row already says 'possibly the same man as the Gregorio arriving 1924'. Born 1881 and 1880, thirteen years apart on the water. A repeat crosser is ordinary and so is a second man of the name; the passenger lists alone cannot tell them apart.
Gregorio LERENA (arr. 1911) || Gregorio LARENA   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED. Both born 1881, landing ten months apart in 1911 and 1912, and LARENA is one of the seven spellings this surname is indexed under. Suggestive and unevidenced, like the pair above - the three Gregorio rows may be one man or three.
Gregorio LERENA (arr. 1924) || Gregorio LARENA   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: THE THIRD SIDE OF THE GREGORIO TRIANGLE, and the same verdict as the other two - born 1880 and 1881, arriving 1924 and 1912. Candidate, not merged: three rows may be one repeat crosser or three men, and a passenger list cannot say which.
Jose M. LERENA || Jose LLERENA (arr. 1910)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: SIX YEARS APART, 1893 against 1887, and nothing shared but the commonest forename in Spanish. Not merged.
Jose M. LERENA || Jose LLERENA (arr. 1926)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: same six-year gap as the line above, and the 1926 man is from LOGRONO while this one has no birthplace at all.
Jose LERENA || Jose Maria LERENA   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWO YEARS APART AND BOTH FROM LOGRONO - which makes them likelier BROTHERS than one man. They belong to the Rioja cluster this archive holds as Spanish and unconnected. Not merged, and not claimed as siblings either: no act names a parent for either.
Jose LERENA || Jose LLERENA (arr. 1926)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWENTY-ONE YEARS APART, 1908 against 1887. Both Logrono, which is the cluster, not the man.
Jose LLERENA (arr. 1910) || Jose LLERENA (arr. 1926)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: STRONGEST OF THE REPEAT-CROSSER CANDIDATES. Both born 1887, both spelled LLERENA, sixteen years apart; the 1926 entry adds LOGRONO. A man who crossed twice fits exactly. Not merged, on the same rule as the Gregorios: an age and a spelling are not an identification.
Jose Ma. LLERENA || Jose Julio LERENA Juanico   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT COUNTRIES AND DIFFERENT FAMILIES - a Madrid man born 1873 against the sixth child of Luis Eugenio Lerena Lenguas and Julia Juanico, born at Montevideo in March 1879.
Jose Julio LERENA Juanico || Jose Loreto LERENA Fernandez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: SEVENTY-THREE YEARS AND THREE GENERATIONS APART. Jose Loreto is Sancho's eighth child, born Canelones 10 December 1806; Jose Julio is Luis Eugenio's son, born 1879.
Josefa LERENA de L. || Josefa LERENA TRAIBEL (1851-1947)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: PROBABLY DIFFERENT WOMEN, and the sharper of the two Josefa questions. The 1895 enumerator writes wives as [maiden surname] "de L." - Lenguas de Lerena, Salvanach de Lerena - so line 4 married a LERENA. Josefa Lerena Traibel married a MORATORIO, on the parish act of her daughters' baptism at Union, 8 Mar 1880. Either the "de L." reading is wrong, or these are two women. Not merged.
Josefa LERENA (godmother, 1885) || Josefa LERENA TRAIBEL (1851-1947)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, HELD APART ON PURPOSE. The Traibel row has claimed this candidacy since 14 Sep and refuses the merge itself, citing the two Arturos. Ages agree within a year. No act joins them.
Juan Jose LERENA || Juan Bernardo LERENA Pelaez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: SPAIN AGAINST URUGUAY. Juan Jose is of the Logrono cluster and crossed at least three times; Juan Bernardo is a child of Bernabe Lerena and Casimira Pelaez, baptised at Trinidad, Flores in August 1871.
Juan LARENA (Gualeguaychu) || Juan Antonio LARENA   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON, and the younger row says so - 'son of Juan Larena and Petrona Lopez'. Head of the Gualeguaychu Larena kindred and his son, born 1837.
Juan LARENA (Gualeguaychu) || Juan Bernardo LERENA Pelaez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWO DIFFERENT KINDREDS IN TWO COUNTRIES - the Gualeguaychu LARENAs of Entre Rios, and the Trinidad LERENAs of Flores.
Juan Antonio LARENA || Juan Facundo Frutuoso BARROS Lerena   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT COUNTRIES AND DIFFERENT SURNAMES - a LARENA of Gualeguaychu born 1837, and a BARROS Lerena baptised at the Cordon, Montevideo in November 1831, grandson of Sancho.
Juan Pablo LERENA || Juan Bernardo LERENA Pelaez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: ARGENTINA AGAINST URUGUAY. Juan Pablo is one of only two Lerena baptisms indexed in all Argentina; Juan Bernardo is Bernabe and Casimira Pelaez's child at Trinidad, Flores, 1871.
Juan Carlos LERENA || Juan Agustin LERENA Fernandez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: NINETY YEARS APART AND THE CENTRAL QUESTION IS NOT THIS. Juan Agustin is Sancho's eldest, born Canelones about 1791; Juan Carlos is Pablo Armando's father, named on the 1908 ficha and in no other record anywhere.
Juan Carlos LERENA || Juan Carlos GONZALEZ LERENA (m. 1920s, Montevideo)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CHECKED AND EXCLUDED ON 15 SEPTEMBER, and the exclusion stands - the Gonzalez Lerena man is the only 'Juan Carlos Lerena' the indexes hold, and he is not Pablo Armando's father. Kept apart deliberately; merging them would invent the answer to this archive's central question.
Juan Bernardo LERENA Pelaez || Juan Carlos GONZALEZ LERENA (m. 1920s, Montevideo)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN - a Trinidad child of 1871 and a Montevideo groom of the 1920s.
Juan Facundo Frutuoso BARROS Lerena || Juan Agustin LERENA Fernandez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: UNCLE AND NEPHEW, forty years apart. Juan Agustin is Sancho's eldest son (1791); Juan Facundo is Sancho's GRANDSON, baptised at the Cordon in 1831.
Juan Agustin LERENA Fernandez || Juan Carlos GONZALEZ LERENA (m. 1920s, Montevideo)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: a century and a half apart - 1791 against a marriage of the 1920s.

Luis LLERENA || Don Luis LERENA (the godfather) = probably Luis LERENA y Fernandez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN. The godfather was born 1811 at Montevideo and stood by proxy at Rosario in 1866; the other lived at Rosario and married Clementina Baranda.
Luis LLERENA || Luis LERENA (son of Cayetana Faustina)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: ROSARIO AGAINST TRINIDAD. The Rosario Luis married Clementina Baranda; the other is a child baptised at Trinidad, Flores on 19 August 1895 to Cayetana Faustina Lerena with no father named.
Don Luis Eugenio LERENA LENGUAS || Don Luis LERENA (the godfather) = probably Luis LERENA y Fernandez   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON. The son's marriage act of 19 Dec 1864 names him "hijo legitimo de Don Luis y de Dona Justiniana Lenguas"; Laura Adela's 1869 baptism names the same pair as paternal grandparents. The 29 shared terms are prose, not evidence. But both rows carry 9 FEBRUARY as a birth day 34 years apart, and the son's birth-to-baptism gap is eleven months where the father's was one day - the son's date wants re-reading.
Don Luis Eugenio LERENA LENGUAS || Luis LERENA (son of Luis Lerena Lenguas)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON, and the son's own row says so - 'hijo legitimo', died at Montevideo on 12 December 1897 aged twenty-five and unmarried. Twenty-seven years apart.
Don Luis Eugenio LERENA LENGUAS || Luis LERENA (son of Cayetana Faustina)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FIFTY YEARS APART and different branches - 1845 in the Lerena Lenguas trunk against an 1895 Trinidad baptism with no father named.
Don Luis Eugenio LERENA LENGUAS || Luis Felipe LERENA Salvanach   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: UNCLE AND NEPHEW. Luis Eugenio and Gilberto (1854) were brothers, so Gilberto's son of 1892 is his nephew - and named LUIS for the same grandfather, which is why the forename repeats.
Don Luis LERENA (the godfather) = probably Luis LERENA y Fernandez || Luis LERENA (son of Luis Lerena Lenguas)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: GREAT-GRANDFATHER AND GREAT-GRANDSON, sixty-one years apart - Don Luis born 1811, and Luis Eugenio's son born 1872.
Don Luis LERENA (the godfather) = probably Luis LERENA y Fernandez || Luis Felipe LERENA Salvanach   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: EIGHTY-ONE YEARS APART - and the younger was NAMED FOR the elder, which the Salvanach row says outright: 'named LUIS, for his grandfather Don Luis Lerena'. A namesake, not a duplicate.
Luis LERENA (son of Luis Lerena Lenguas) || Luis Felipe LERENA Salvanach   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FIRST COUSINS. Luis Eugenio's son of 1872 and Gilberto's son of 1892, both named for the same grandfather.
Luis LERENA (son of Cayetana Faustina) || Luis Felipe LERENA Salvanach   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TRINIDAD AGAINST BUENOS AIRES, and three years apart. No connection but the name.

Maria LERENA || Maria Julia Margarita Josefa Emirena Justiniana LERENA Juanico (m. HERRERA)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: *** AN EXCLUSION ON THE CENTRAL QUESTION, AND THE REGISTER ALREADY MAKES IT. *** Maria Lerena is PABLO ARMANDO'S MOTHER, named on his 1908 enrolment card. Maria Julia is the only Lerena baptised at Rosario between 1731 and 1901, which makes her the obvious candidate and she is not: she was SIXTEEN in February 1882 when Pablo Armando was born, and four years off marrying JAIME HERRERA at Montevideo. Kept apart deliberately - merging them would answer this archive's central question by accident.

Maria LERENA || Maria LERENA (m. She married a CARDOZO, where Pablo Armando's mother stands beside JUAN CARLOS LERENA. Not merged: this archive has twice built on a Maria it had not pinned down.

Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena || Maria de las Mercedes MORATORIO Lerena   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: SISTERS, probably twins, baptised together at Union on 8 Mar 1880. They share an ark because ONE ACT NAMES BOTH - the strongest signal this gate has is also its most ambiguous. A shared ark means a shared record, not a shared person. Separate godmothers.
Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena || Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT WOMEN - child of Fernando Moratorio and Josefa Lerena Traibel, Montevideo, 1880; against Uruguay. The forename repeats across this whole surname; this register holds a dozen Marias.
Maria de las Mercedes MORATORIO Lerena || Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT WOMEN - child of Fernando Moratorio and Josefa Lerena Traibel, Montevideo, 1880; against Uruguay. The forename repeats across this whole surname; this register holds a dozen Marias.
Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO) || Maria LLERENA (Espanola, 60, head of Casa 2)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: A URUGUAYAN OF THE TRINIDAD KINDRED against a Spanish-born head of household in Montevideo.
Pedro LLERENA || Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED. Both are Uruguayan men fathering children in the 1880s - one married Sofia Clavijo and had a daughter in 1884, the other is named as already dead on his son Victor's death registration of 1889. Nothing places them together and nothing rules it out; LLERENA and LORENA are both on this family's list of seven spellings.
Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho) || Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, AND AN OLD ONE. Sancho's son was born 31 July 1793; Victor's father was dead by 1889 and Victor was born 1861, so his father could be a man born in the 1790s fathering late, or a generation younger. Not merged - see the Fidel pair, which turns on the same registration.
Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho) || Pedro LERENA Vega (b. 1828)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON, and the younger row says so - 'a son of Pedro Antonio Lerena and Vicenta Vega'. Twenty-four years apart.
Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889) || Pedro LERENA Vega (b. 1828)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED. A man born about 1817 could be the father of a Victor born 1861 and be dead by 1889. Nothing names Vicenta Vega's son as Victor's father, and nothing excludes him.
Ramon LLERENA || Ramon LERENA (of Rosario-Colla, fl. 1794-1820)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, NOT MERGED. One is 'most likely Gabino's father' and the other heads the Rosario-Colla household as Mauricio's father, flourishing about 1800-1820. Two Ramons in one small Uruguayan orbit, and no act joins them.
Roque Luis Armando LERENA || ROQUE LARENA   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWENTY-FOUR YEARS AND TWO COUNTRIES APART - and the younger is the ELDEST SON of this archive's subject, born at Cape Town on 25 November 1905. The other was born in Entre Rios about 1881, within a year of Pablo Armando. *** The interest is the FORENAME, not the person: *** Pablo Armando gave his first son the name ROQUE, and here it is on a man of his own age and country. Recorded as a name, not a relative.
Josefa LERENA (godmother, 1885) || Josefa LERENA de L.   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: CANDIDATE, HELD APART ON PURPOSE. The 1895 household file calls the widow of line 4 "almost certainly" this godmother, and the ages fit - born about 1852, of Gilberto's own generation, in the house of the man whose son she had stood for in 1885. No act joins them: the godmother is a name in a font entry and nothing more.
Gilberto LERENA (Gilberto Justiniano LERENA LENGUAS) || Gilberto LERENA (b.1916, arr. 1928)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: SIXTY-TWO YEARS APART. The Stud Book Gilberto was 41 in the 1895 census; the other is a twelve-year-old estudiante on a 1928 passenger list. If the 1928 identification holds they are great-grandfather and great-grandson; if it does not they are unrelated namesakes. Not one man either way. They share terms only because both rows now cite the same parish index.
Gilberto LERENA (b.1916, arr. 1928) || Gilberto LERENA Salvanach (the younger, b.1878)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: THIRTY-EIGHT YEARS APART, and the CEMLA list records them as father and son travelling together. The shared terms - cipriana, corazon, capital - are the cross-reference written on 20 September, not evidence of one person.
Carmen LERENA Pelaez || Carmen LERENA (d. 1841, aged three)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: THIRTY-NINE YEARS AND A DEPARTMENT APART. The Pelaez child was born about 1877 at Trinidad, Flores, to Bernabe Lerena and Casimira Pelaez. The other was buried at the Cementerio del Cordon, Montevideo, on 14 August 1841, aged three, daughter of Justo Lerena and Juana Quintana. The shared terms are the word "child" and the word "surname".
Maria E. C. LERENA || Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT WOMEN, eleven years and a country apart. Maria E. C. is the soltera of 26 on line 5 of the 1895 Buenos Aires schedule, born 1869; the Moratorio girl was baptised at Union, Montevideo in March 1880. The shared terms are words added to the Moratorio note on 20 September, not evidence.
Maria Ema LERENA LENGUAS || Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT WOMEN and different branches - a LERENA LENGUAS against a MORATORIO LERENA, whose mother was Josefa Lerena Traibel. Same cause: prose added on 20 September, three common words in it.
Avelino Carlos LERENA (b.1904, Mendoza) || Avelino LERENA Fernandez (son of Sancho)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: EIGHTY-NINE YEARS APART. Sancho's son was born 1815 in Uruguay and heads the LERENA TRAIBEL branch; the other was baptised at Mendoza in 1904. Three generations between them.
Carlos Augusto Federico LERENA Salvanach (b. 9 July 1883) || Carlos MARTINEZ Lerena   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT FAMILIES. A LERENA Salvanach against a MARTINEZ Lerena, whose mother was the Carolina Lerena of the Martinez marriage. Shared terms are prose.
Carlos Augusto Federico LERENA Salvanach (b. 9 July 1883) || Carlos LERENA (of Mendoza, m. Adela CAMIGLIA)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWO MEN, ONE NAME - and this register said so on the Rosa Maria Eppens row on 14 September. Her Carlos is Gilberto's son, SOLTERO at thirty in the Retiro act of 1914 and married to her; Adela Camiglia's Carlos is at MENDOZA and fathered Avelino Carlos in 1904. Kept apart on purpose, and the Mendoza man now has a row so the relationship edges can point somewhere true.
Maria E. C. LERENA || Maria Angelica Julia LERENA Salvanach   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: NOT GILBERTO'S DAUGHTER AND NOT THIS CHILD. Maria E. C. is the SOLTERA OF TWENTY-SIX on line 5 of the 1895 schedule - the archive retracted "daughter of Gilberto" for her on 14 September, because it would have made him fifteen. Maria Angelica Julia is eight on line 10. They share a schedule, not a person.
Maria E. C. LERENA || Maria Carlota LERENA Salvanach   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWENTY-SIX AGAINST SIX on the same schedule, and the elder is not a daughter of the house at all - more likely Josefa's daughter or Gilberto's sister, as the household file says.
Avelino Carlos LERENA (b.1904, Mendoza) || Avelino Eduardo LERENA Traibel   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: AN ARGENTINE OF 1904 AGAINST A URUGUAYAN OF THE 1840s. Avelino Eduardo is Avelino and Ambrosia Traibel's son, fathering children from 1865; the other was baptised at Mendoza in 1904 to Carlos Lerena and Adela Camiglia.
Avelino Carlos LERENA (b.1904, Mendoza) || Avelino Pedro Joaquin Silvestre LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: THIRTY-NINE YEARS AND TWO COUNTRIES APART - a Union, Montevideo christening of March 1866 against a Mendoza baptism of 1904.
Avelino LERENA Fernandez (son of Sancho) || Avelino Eduardo LERENA Traibel   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON, and that is the finding of 20 September rather than an assumption - two parish acts give Avelino Eduardo's father as Avelino with Ambrosia Traibel on the record. The forename repeating across the generation is exactly what made the branch ambiguous in the first place.
Avelino LERENA Fernandez (son of Sancho) || Avelino Pedro Joaquin Silvestre LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: GRANDFATHER AND GRANDSON. Avelino b.1815; his son Avelino Eduardo's child christened at Union on 5 March 1866. THREE Avelinos in three generations, which is why this branch took a week to place.
Avelino Eduardo LERENA Traibel || Avelino Pedro Joaquin Silvestre LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON - the christening of 5 March 1866 at Union names them as such.
Pedro LLERENA || Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN - Pedro Llerena married Sofia Clavijo and fathered a daughter in 1884, when Pedro Gualberto was fifteen.
Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho) || Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: GREAT-GRANDFATHER AND GREAT-GRANDSON, seventy-six years apart - Pedro Antonio born 31 July 1793, Pedro Gualberto born 19 October 1869.
Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889) || Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN. Victor's father was dead by 1889; Pedro Gualberto was born in 1869 and married in 1921.
Pedro LERENA Vega (b. 1828) || Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FIFTY-TWO YEARS APART and different branches - Pedro Antonio and Vicenta Vega's son against Avelino Eduardo and Celedonia Villademoros's.
Alejandro LERENA TRAIBEL (formerly recorded as LERENA LENGUAS) || Alejandro M. LERENA (m.1 Elisa VILLADEMOROS; m.2 Julia BASSANI)   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: *** STRONG CANDIDATE, DELIBERATELY NOT MERGED. *** Alejandro Lerena Traibel is Avelino and Ambrosia's eldest, born 29 May 1843 or 1845; the other fathered a son at the Cordon in 1873, which fits a man of twenty-seven or twenty-nine. And his brother AVELINO EDUARDO demonstrably married a VILLADEMOROS, which is what makes two brothers marrying two sisters the obvious reading. *** But the 1873 act does not name this Alejandro's parents, *** and this archive does not join a man to a branch on a surname and an age. TEST: the marriage act, or any record giving his father.
Alejandro LERENA TRAIBEL (formerly recorded as LERENA LENGUAS) || Alejandro Joaquin Narciso LERENA Villardemoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: TWENTY-NINE YEARS APART. If the candidate identification above holds they are father and son; if it does not they are unrelated namesakes. Either way not one man.
Alejandro M. LERENA (m.1 Elisa VILLADEMOROS; m.2 Julia BASSANI) || Alejandro Joaquin Narciso LERENA Villardemoros   # REVIEWED 20 Sep: FATHER AND SON - the christening of 29 May 1873 names them as such, and the son carries the father's forename.
Pedro LLERENA || Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: A CENTURY APART. Pedro Antonio was born 31 July 1793 and is Sancho's second son; Pedro Llerena married Sofia Clavijo and registered children in Uruguay between 1895 and 1903. The shared terms are words added to Pedro Llerena's note on 21 September when his five extra children were found.
Cipriano LERENA (of Trinidad) || Cipriano LERENA Montana   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT PARENTS, AND A CANDIDATE PAIR THIS ARCHIVE SHOULD WATCH. The Trinidad Cipriano married Lauriana Beloqui and fathered children there in the 1870s and 1880s; Cipriano Lerena Montana's 1928 death registration names his parents as Mauricio Lerena and Casimira Montano. Both are in the Flores orbit and the forename is not common, so they may be kin - but a shared forename and a shared department is not an identification, and neither is entered as the other. TEST: the Trinidad Cipriano's own parentage, which no record here gives.
Juan Carlos LERENA || Juan Facundo Frutuoso BARROS Lerena   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT MEN AND DIFFERENT SURNAMES. Juan Facundo is Sancho's grandson through Justa Pastora, a BARROS Lerena baptised at the Cordon on 13 December 1831 and dead at Montevideo on 3 May 1915; Juan Carlos is Pablo Armando's father, who carries LERENA as the paternal surname and appears in no record of his own. The shared terms are words added to Juan Facundo's row on 21 September.
Juan Bernardo LERENA Pelaez || Juan Facundo Frutuoso BARROS Lerena   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: FORTY YEARS AND TWO BRANCHES APART. Juan Bernardo is Bernabe Lerena and Casimira Pelaez's child, baptised at Trinidad, Flores in August 1871; Juan Facundo is Justa Pastora's son by Manuel Zeferino Barros, baptised at the Cordon in December 1831 and dead at Montevideo in May 1915.
Juan Facundo Frutuoso BARROS Lerena || Juan Arturo LERENA Traibel   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT BRANCHES, THIRTY YEARS APART. Juan Facundo is Justa Pastora's son by Manuel Zeferino Barros, baptised at the Cordon 13 December 1831 and dead at Montevideo 3 May 1915; Juan Arturo is Avelino's son by Ambrosia Traibel, born 16 February 1861 and christened at the Cordon 31 December 1861. The shared terms are the parish and the month, and both notes were written on the same day.
Juan Arturo LERENA Traibel || Juan Agustin LERENA Fernandez   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: UNCLE AND GREAT-NEPHEW, SEVENTY YEARS APART. Juan Agustin is Sancho's eldest, born about 1791 at el Canelon and in the padron of 1812; Juan Arturo is Sancho's grandson through Avelino, born 16 February 1861.
Maria E. C. LERENA || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT COUNTRIES AND DIFFERENT HOUSEHOLDS. Maria E. C. is a soltera of 26 in Gilberto's Buenos Aires household at the 1895 census; Maria Adelina lived and died at Montevideo, married Carlos Alberto Fein and was buried on certificate 48 of 1946. Both are flagged as birth-year problems, which is why the words match.
Maria LORENA || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: A ONE-YEAR-OLD IN ROSARIO IS NOT A MONTEVIDEO BRIDE. Maria Lorena is the infant daughter of AGUSTIN LORENA the Peruvian tinsmith, aged 1 at the 1869 Rosario census, born Cordoba, Argentina; Maria Adelina is Avelino and Ambrosia's daughter, born and died at Montevideo. The birth years are within six because one of them is a guess flagged as not a finding.
Maria Julia Margarita Josefa Emirena Justiniana LERENA Juanico (m. HERRERA) || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT MOTHERS. The Juanico daughter is Luis Lerena y Lenguas's child by Maria Juanico; Maria Adelina is Avelino's child by Ambrosia Traibel. First cousins once removed at most, and married into different families.
Maria Luisa Lina LERENA Gazo || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: THE COMPOUND SURNAME IS THE ANSWER. Lerena Gazo names a mother GAZO; Lerena Traibel names a mother TRAIBEL. The shared terms are the words "child", "civil" and "registration", which every Uruguayan civil-registration row here carries.
Maria Leonor LERENA Beloqui || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT MOTHERS AND DIFFERENT DEPARTMENTS. Lerena Beloqui belongs to the Trinidad, Flores group through Lauriana Beloqui; Maria Adelina is Montevideo and Traibel.
Maria Regina LERENA Barragan || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT MOTHERS. Barragan against Traibel, and no record joins the two households.
Maria Carlota LERENA Salvanach || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: GILBERTO'S DAUGHTER, NOT AVELINO'S. Maria Carlota is a child of Gilberto Lerena and Josefa Salvanach in Buenos Aires; Maria Adelina is a child of Avelino Lerena and Ambrosia Traibel at Montevideo.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: AUNT AND NIECE. Maria Carlota Moratorio Lerena was baptised at the Union on 8 March 1880, daughter of Fernando Moratorio and Josefa Lerena Traibel - and Josefa is Maria Adelina's sister. They share the word "Avelino" because he is grandfather to one and father to the other.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT HUSBANDS AND DIFFERENT DECADES. The Cardozo marriage belongs to the pair of Lerena siblings who married a pair of Cardozo siblings and produced the 1911 dispensation; Maria Adelina married Carlos Alberto Fein.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria LLERENA (Espanola, 60, head of Casa 2)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: A SPANISH-BORN HEAD OF HOUSE IS NOT A MONTEVIDEO DAUGHTER. The Casa 2 woman is enumerated ESPANOLA and aged 60; Maria Adelina was born at Montevideo about 1865 to a Uruguayan father and mother.
Mercedes LORENA || Mercedes Micaela LERENA Traibel   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: ANOTHER OF AGUSTIN LORENA'S ROSARIO CHILDREN. Mercedes Lorena is aged 9 at the 1869 Rosario census, born Cordoba, Argentina, daughter of the Peruvian tinsmith; Mercedes Micaela was born 8 May 1863 at Montevideo and christened at the Union in 1866 with Sancho and Maria Fernandez named as her grandparents.
Mercedes Micaela LERENA Traibel || Mercedes LERENA (godmother, 1880)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: THE SAME WOMAN IS LIKELY AND IS NOT PROVED - AND THE OBSTACLE IS HER AGE. Mercedes Micaela was born 8 May 1863, so she was SIXTEEN at the Union font in March 1880. The parish, the family and the name all fit, and the child was named for the godmother. But a sixteen-year-old godmother is an inference; the 1880 act would have to give her age or her parents, and the transcript read here gives neither. Kept apart deliberately - see both notes.
Pedro LLERENA || Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT WIVES AND DIFFERENT DEPARTMENTS. Pedro Llerena married Sofia Clavijo and registered six children in Colonia between 1884 and 1903; Pedro Alberto Ysabelino was baptised at the Aguada in 1852, married Maria Ines Larriera and died at Montevideo in 1921.
Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho) || Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: GODFATHER AND GODSON, UNCLE AND NEPHEW - AND THE BOY IS NAMED FOR THE MAN. Pedro Antonio was born 31 July 1793 and stood at the font on 21 November 1852 with his wife Vicenta Vega; the child he held was his brother Avelino's son, born 16 March 1852. They appear in one sentence of one act, which is exactly why the words match.
Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921) || Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT PARENTS ON TWO DOCUMENTS. Pedro Lorena's son's 1889 registration gives Pedro's own parents as FIDEL LERENA and MARIA RUEDAS; the Aguada act of 1852 gives Pedro Alberto Ysabelino's as AVELINO LERENA and AMBROSIA TRAIBEL. If the Fidel is Sancho's son they are first cousins, which is not established.
Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921) || Pedro LERENA Vega (b. 1828)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: FIRST COUSINS, THIRTY-FIVE YEARS APART. Pedro Lerena Vega is Pedro Antonio and Vicenta Vega's own son, born about 1817; Pedro Alberto Ysabelino is Avelino's son, born 16 March 1852 - and the older man's parents stood godparents to the younger.
Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921) || Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: UNCLE AND NEPHEW. Pedro Gualberto was christened at the Cordon on 15 January 1870, son of Avelino Eduardo Lerena and Celedonia Villademoros - and Avelino Eduardo is Pedro Alberto Ysabelino's brother.
Maria Ema LERENA LENGUAS || Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: COUSINS, DIFFERENT MOTHERS. Maria Ema is a daughter of Luis Lerena y Fernandez and Justiniana Lenguas; Maria Adelina was born 13 August 1865 to Avelino Lerena and Ambrosia Traibel and baptised at the Cordon on 31 December 1865. The shared terms are words added to Maria Adelina's row on 21 September when her act was read.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria de las Mercedes MORATORIO Lerena   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: AUNT AND NIECE, AND THE SURNAMES SAY SO. Maria de las Mercedes is Fernando Moratorio and Josefa Lerena Traibel's daughter, baptised at the Union on 8 March 1880; Maria Adelina is Josefa's youngest sister, born 13 August 1865. They share the word "Carlota" because a Carlota Munoz stood godmother to one and a sister named Maria Carlota was baptised beside the other.
Alberto Octavio LERENA LENGUAS || Alberto Julian LERENA LARRIERA   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: FIRST COUSINS ONCE REMOVED, AND THIRTY-THREE YEARS APART. Alberto Octavio was baptised at Montevideo on 7 October 1850, son of Luis and Justiniana Lenguas; Alberto Julian was born in the Departamento de San Jose on 17 August 1883, son of Alberto Lerena and Maria Ines Larriera. The shared terms come from the note added to Alberto Octavio's row on 21 September WITHDRAWING him as a candidate to be that Alberto.
Carlos Augusto Federico LERENA Salvanach (b. 9 July 1883) || Carlos Ambrosio Florencio LERENA LARRIERA   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT MOTHERS, DIFFERENT COUNTRIES. Carlos Augusto Federico is Gilberto's son by Josefa Salvanach at Buenos Aires; Carlos Ambrosio Florencio was christened at San Jose, Uruguay on 22 June 1889 to Alberto Lerena and Maria Ines Larriera. Both are named for a Carlos and that is all they share.
Carlos Ambrosio Florencio LERENA LARRIERA || Carlos LERENA (of Mendoza, m. Adela CAMIGLIA)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: MENDOZA IS NOT SAN JOSE. The Mendoza Carlos married Adela Camiglia in Argentina; this child was christened at San Jose, Uruguay in 1889 with his parents named on the act.
Maria Luisa LERENA || Maria Adelina LERENA LARRIERA   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: GILBERTO'S DAUGHTER IN BUENOS AIRES, NOT ALBERTO'S IN SAN JOSE. The two households are in different countries and the parents are documented separately - Gilberto Lerena with Josefa Salvanach at Buenos Aires, Alberto Lerena with Maria Ines Larriera in the Departamento de San Jose.

Avelino LERENA Fernandez (son of Sancho) || Avelino LERENA Villademoros (d. 1914)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: GRANDFATHER AND GRANDSON, AND THE GRANDFATHER DIED FIRST. Avelino Lerena Fernandez was born 1815 and died 22 August 1890 at La Paz, Canelones, son of Sancho; this man died at Montevideo on 5 January 1914 aged 45, and his own parents are given as Avelino Eduardo Lerena and Celedonia Villademoro.
Avelino Eduardo LERENA Traibel || Avelino LERENA Villademoros (d. 1914)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: FATHER AND SON. The 1914 death registration names Avelino Eduardo as this man's father, with Celedonia Villademoro as his mother. THREE AVELINOS IN THREE GENERATIONS is exactly the shape that made this archive spend two days in September deciding whether Avelino married twice - and the parish itself wrote the disambiguation at the Cordon in 1861, "Avelino Lerena (hijo)".
Avelino Pedro Joaquin Silvestre LERENA Villademoros || Avelino LERENA Villademoros (d. 1914)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: BROTHERS, PROBABLY, AND A DATE THAT WILL NOT BEND. Both are sons of Avelino Eduardo and Celedonia. Avelino Pedro Joaquin Silvestre was born December 1865 and christened at the Union on 5 March 1866, and carries a MARRIAGE REGISTRATION OF 1921; this man died on 5 January 1914. A man who died in 1914 does not marry in 1921, so they are not one person unless that index card belongs to somebody else. The ages differ by four years and both come from weak fields. TEST: the 1921 marriage registration.
Pedro LLERENA || Pedro Segundo LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT WIVES AND DIFFERENT DEPARTMENTS. Pedro Llerena married Sofia Clavijo and registered children in Colonia from 1884; Pedro Segundo married Paulina Prando Algar and died at Montevideo in 1936.
Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho) || Pedro Segundo LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: GREAT-GRANDFATHER'S BROTHER AND GREAT-GRAND-NEPHEW, SEVENTY-SEVEN YEARS APART. Pedro Antonio was born 31 July 1793; Pedro Segundo died in 1936 aged 66.
Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921) || Pedro Segundo LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: UNCLE AND NEPHEW. Pedro Alberto Ysabelino is Avelino Fernandez's son, born 16 March 1852; Pedro Segundo is his brother Avelino Eduardo's son, born about 1870. Both were named Pedro for the same great-uncle, Pedro Antonio Lerena, who stood godfather to the elder of them.
Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889) || Pedro Segundo LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: DIFFERENT PARENTS ON TWO DOCUMENTS. Pedro Lorena's parents are FIDEL LERENA and MARIA RUEDAS on his son's 1889 registration; Pedro Segundo's are Avelino Eduardo Lerena and Celedonia Villademoros on his own of 1936.
Pedro Segundo LERENA Villademoros || Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: THE LIKELIEST MERGE ON THIS PAGE, AND IT IS NOT MADE. Same parents, same first name, same birth year - Pedro Gualberto born 19 October 1869 and christened at the Cordon on 15 January 1870; Pedro Segundo dead at Montevideo on 23 August 1936 aged 66, which gives 1869 or 1870. Either one man recorded with two second forenames, or two brothers both called Pedro - and in THIS family two brothers have already turned out to share a birthday five years apart, so the second reading is not absurd. TEST: the 1921 marriage registration indexed for Pedro Gualberto, against the 1936 death act for Pedro Segundo and his wife Paulina Prando Algar.

Dr. D. Damaso LARRANAGA (Vicar, 1833) || Dr. D. Juan Ciriaco OTAEGUI (priest, 1833)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: TWO DIFFERENT CLERGYMEN ON ONE ACT, AND THE GATE FLAGGED THEM ONLY BECAUSE BOTH BEGIN "Dr. D." The act of 19 October 1833 reads "yo el D.r D.n JUAN CIRIACO OTAEGUI, con licencia del Cura y Vicario D.r D. DAMASO LARRANAGA" - Otaegui performed the baptism and signed it; Larranaga held the office that licensed it. The shared record and the shared honorific are the whole of the resemblance. Larranaga is Damaso Antonio Larranaga, first Vicar Apostolic of Montevideo.
# RETIRED 11 October 2026. This pair asked whether the grandson and 'Don Domingo RODRIGUEZ (the elder, of la Colonia)' were one person, and answered that they were grandfather and grandson. THE GRANDFATHER DOES NOT EXIST: the plate of the 1840 act reads 'Nieto Pat.o de D. JOAQUIN RODRIG.z', and the forename Domingo had been supplied in square brackets by a reader. The row is withdrawn. The pair is left here, struck out, because a deleted line teaches nobody: Domingo RODRIGUEZ DE CARVALLO || Don Domingo RODRIGUEZ (the elder, of la Colonia)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: GRANDFATHER AND GRANDSON, ON ONE ACT. The baptism of 25 October 1840 reads "bautice solemnem.te a DOMINGO que nacio el cuatro de Agosto del ppte. ano, hijo legitimo de D. ANTONIO RODRIGUEZ DE CARBALLO ... Nieto pat[erno de D. Domingo] RODRIG.Z y D.a JUANA DEL ESPIRITU S.TO, ambos nat.s de la COLONIA". The child is a grandson of Sancho through Dominga Lerena; the elder Domingo is the other grandfather and belongs to no Lerena line. The child carries his name, which is why one act holds two Domingos.
Juan Carlos LERENA || Juan Carlos LLERENA (b. Buenos Aires 1910)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: THE PAIR THIS ARCHIVE MUST BE MOST CAREFUL WITH, AND IT IS SETTLED BY ARITHMETIC. Juan Carlos Lerena is Pablo Armando's father, named on the enrolment card of 1908; Pablo Armando was born at Rosario de Santa Fe on 22 February 1882, so his father was an adult by 1881. The Juan Carlos Llerena of Buenos Aires was BAPTISED ON THE DAY HE WAS BORN'S YEAR - 16 July 1910 - son of Rafael Llerena, twenty-nine, and Maria Carlota Amadeo Lastra, twenty-seven. He was two years old when the enrolment card was written. He cannot be the father and nothing here suggests he is kin at all; he is kept so that the next search for a Juan Carlos Llerena meets a dated row instead of a fresh hope.

Juan Carlos GONZALEZ LERENA (m. 1920s, Montevideo) || Juan Carlos LLERENA (b. Buenos Aires 1910)   # REVIEWED 21 Sep: TWO JUAN CARLOSES, TWO COUNTRIES, AND NEITHER IS THE ONE. THAT THIS ARCHIVE NOW HOLDS TWO JUAN CARLOSES WHO ARE BOTH RULED OUT IS THE POINT OF HOLDING THEM: the forename recurs in this surname, which is exactly why finding one proves nothing.

LERENA (m.1 Elisa VILLADEMOROS; m.2 Julia BASSANI)   # *** REVIEWED AGAIN 27 Sep 2026: THE GAP IS NOW ZERO ON PARENTAGE AND ONE YEAR ON BIRTH. *** Alejandro M. Lerena's parents are AVELINO LERENA and AMBROSIA TRAIBEL, read off film 007713388 image 02455 - the SAME COUPLE this man was baptised to at La Mauricia on 7 January 1846, and the SAME FORENAME. His own stated ages put his birth between March and November 1844; the baptism act offers 29 May 1843 or 1845. *** NOTHING NOW SEPARATES THEM EXCEPT THE POSSIBILITY OF TWO SONS OF ONE COUPLE SHARING A FORENAME ***, which happens when the first dies and nothing here shows the 1846 infant died. *** STILL NOT MERGED, AND THE REASON IS DISCIPLINE, NOT DOUBT: *** this archive has merged on less and been wrong, and the merge should be done in one deliberate pass. What would close it outright: a burial for an infant Alejandro Lerena 1846-1860, which would mean TWO sons; or any act naming Alejandro M. Lerena with a birth date.

LERENA (with Julia BASSANI)   # SAME MAN, PROVED 27 Sep 2026, AND THIS PAIR IS NOW A MERGE WAITING TO BE DONE ... THIS LINE IS A DEBT, NOT A JUDGEMENT.' The two rows are now one: 'Alejandro M. LERENA (m.1 Elisa VILLADEMOROS; m.2 Julia BASSANI)'. It is kept as a comment so the pair gate's baseline count still makes sense and so nobody re-splits them.
Alejandro Joaquin Narciso LERENA Villardemoros || Alejandro M. LERENA (m.1 Elisa VILLADEMOROS; m.2 Julia BASSANI)   # REVIEWED 22 Sep 2026: DIFFERENT MEN, a generation apart. One born 29 Oct 1872; the other was forty-two years old in November 1886 and so born in 1844.
Emilia LERENA || Emilia Alberta LERENA Bassani   # REVIEWED 22 Sep 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN. One b.1886 in SPAIN, in Leandro's 1895 Buenos Aires household; the other b. 16 March 1888 at calle Ybirini N 321, MONTEVIDEO, daughter of Alejandro M. Lerena and Julia Bassani, IMAGE READ. Two countries, and the Montevideo one was still an infant when the census took the other.
Jacinta LERENA || Jacinta Mercedes Gregoria LERENA Villademoros   # REVIEWED 27 Sep 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, and the near-miss is worth stating because this family really did cross. Jacinta Mercedes Gregoria was born 19 September 1876 at MONTEVIDEO and baptised at the Cordon, daughter of Alejandro M. Lerena and Elisa Villademoros. The other Jacinta is in the 1895 Argentine census at FEDERAL, ENTRE RIOS, born about 1881. Five years apart, and a birth at Montevideo in 1876 cannot be a birth in Entre Rios in 1881. *** BUT NOTE WHAT MAKES THIS PAIR TEMPTING: *** Alejandro M. Lerena's later family IS Argentine - a son born in Misiones in 1891 and another at Buenos Aires in 1895 - so a daughter of his in Entre Rios is the kind of thing this archive should expect to find. It is not this one.
Fernando MORATORIO (the elder) || Fernando MORATORIO (the younger)   # REVIEWED 28 Sep 2026: FATHER AND SON, ON ONE ACT. The two Catedral Basilica baptisms of 8 March 1880 name the elder as the PATERNAL GRANDFATHER of the children and the younger as their FATHER - so both men stand on the same opening, one generation apart, and the act itself distinguishes them. The elder's wife is NICOLASA TORRES; the younger's is JOSEFA LERENA TRAIBEL. *** THE YOUNGER WAS ENTERED ONLY ON 28 SEPTEMBER 2026 *** - his parents, his wife and both his daughters were already in this register and he was not, which is why the pair never arose before.
MARIA MERCEDES (daughter of Juana, enslaved) || Maria Mercedes ROZZETTI (wife of Eduardo LERENA)   # REVIEWED 7 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, and the only thing they share is a very common pair of forenames. MARIA MERCEDES was born into slavery in Sancho Llerena's Montevideo household on 24 September 1812 and baptised at two days old; the act gives her a mother, JUANA, and no surname, because the household that held her did not give her one. MARIA MERCEDES ROZZETTI married Eduardo Lerena in Buenos Aires in the twentieth century and has a surname of her own. *** A CENTURY AND A COUNTRY APART, AND THE GATE IS RIGHT TO ASK: *** this register carries people whose records deny them a surname, and a forename match is all it ever has to go on for them.
Juan Bernardo LERENA Pelaez || Juan LERENA (m. Eduviges Pelaez 1894)   # *** MERGED 9 October 2026 - AND THIS LINE IS KEPT AS THE RECORD OF A RETRACTED SPLIT. *** Declared DIFFERENT PEOPLE earlier the same day, on the stated ground that the dispensation row had 'no age, no birth and no parents'. It has parents now: the baptism of 26 November 1905 at Santisima Trinidad gives 'hijo legitimo de Juan Lerena y Eduviges Pelaez. Abuelos paternos: BERNABE y CASIMIRA PELAEZ', and Juan Bernardo Lerena Pelaez (b. 20 Aug 1871) is the only Juan among that couple's children. The dispensation row is gone; its facts are in the surviving row. See data/corrections.tsv and data/dispensations-answered-1894-1913.tsv. THE REASON THE SPLIT WAS WRONG IS NOT THAT THE REASONING WAS BAD - it was that the evidence arrived a day later.

Antonina LERENA (b. c.1862, dau. of Bernabe; m. Serapio Fernandez 1885) || Antonina LERENA (the elder, m. Juan PELAEZ)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: TWO WOMEN OF ONE NAME AT TRINIDAD, and the ages prove it. *** The elder was already the mother of a THIRTY-YEAR-OLD in 1880 *** (Maria Pelaez, married 6 March 1880, 'hija legitima de Don Juan Pelaez y de Dona Antonina Lerena'), so born near 1830. *** The younger is given as THIRTY-FIVE in her son's birth act of 1897 ***, so born near 1862, and is a daughter of Bernabe Lerena and Casimira Pelaez. Thirty-two years apart and different husbands.
Serapio FERNANDEZ (the elder, d. by 1897) || Serapio FERNANDEZ (m. Antonina Lerena 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: FATHER AND SON, named as such in one document. The Flores birth act of 1897 gives the child's father as Serapio Fernandez, forty-one, and his paternal grandfather as *** 'Don SERAPIO FERNANDEZ, Oriental, FALLECIDO' *** - dead by then. The son carries the father's forename exactly.
Maria LORENA || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, and this is the only one of the seven where the years are the question. Both land on 1868/1869 and both years come from a STATED AGE, not a guess - but one is a child of ONE at the Rosario census of 1869, born at CORDOBA, daughter of AGUSTIN LORENA; the other is a Uruguayan woman of FIFTY-THREE at Flores in 1922, widow of Maximo Pelaez, dispensed to marry him at Trinidad in 1885. An Argentine infant in Santa Fe cannot be a bride in the Banda Oriental sixteen years later with a different father and a different country. The proximity is coincidence, and the gate was right to ask.
Maria Luisa Lina LERENA Gazo || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN. A compound surname naming a mother's family, of the Montevideo branch; the Trinidad Maria has no recorded parents at all. The gate matched on NOTE VOCABULARY ('child', 'church', 'flores', 'surname'), not on anything about the people.
Maria Leonor LERENA Beloqui || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, on the same ground as the Gazo pair - a Montevideo compound surname against an unparented Trinidad woman. Shared terms are note vocabulary.
Maria Regina LERENA Barragan || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, same ground again. The gate is firing on the words these rows are written in, which is what it does when a row acquires a long note.
Maria Ema LERENA LENGUAS || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN. Christened 27 June 1857 at San Francisco de Asis, Montevideo, a sister of Luis Eugenio - twelve years older than the Trinidad Maria and of the other family entirely. Indexed as 'Maria Ema SERENA Lenguas', which is the same S-for-L trap that put SERENA on the Trinidad woman in 1922 - the spelling is shared and the woman is not.

Maria LLERENA (Espanola, 60, head of Casa 2) || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, and three generations apart. The Casa 2 woman is SPANISH-BORN and sixty, standing in Sancho's generation or the one above it; the Trinidad Maria is ORIENTAL and born about 1869. Shared terms 'birth', 'oriental', 'surname', 'would' are the vocabulary of two long notes.
Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO) || Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885)   # DECLARED 9 Oct 2026 BEFORE THE GATE ASKED, because this is the pair that actually matters and an argument now rests on it. *** THE CHURCH'S OWN ARITHMETIC SEPARATES THEM. *** The Cardozo Maria is a SISTER of Bernabe Lerena, per the kinship diagram of 1911. If she were also the mother of Vitalino Gregorio Pelaez, then he and Florinda Juana Lerena - Bernabe's daughter - would be FIRST COUSINS, and their 1913 impediment would be the SECOND degree. The clerk wrote the THIRD. So Vitalino's mother is a Maria Lerena ONE GENERATION FURTHER OUT, and these are two women. A canonical degree excluding an identification is an unusual thing, and it is recorded here so the exclusion can be challenged rather than assumed.
Alejandro LERENA TRAIBEL (formerly recorded as LERENA LENGUAS) || Alejandro LERENA (the elder, m. Margarita Fernandez Alvarez)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT FAMILIES AND DIFFERENT CENTURIES OF BIRTH. The Montevideo Alejandros belong to the Lenguas/Traibel/Villardemoros branch and are born 1843, 1844 and 1872. The Trinidad Alejandros are a generation or two above that: the elder had a daughter born about 1832, so he belongs to the 1790s or 1800s, and his son was already a WIDOWER remarrying in 1854. The gate matched on NOTE VOCABULARY - 'birth', 'image', 'found', 'first', 'december' - and on the forename, which is the whole of what they share.

Without the 1854 document those look like one man with two wives, and the archive would have merged a father into his son. *** THE SON'S OWN BIRTH YEAR IS UNKNOWN AND IS NOT GUESSED FROM HIS WIDOWHOOD. ***
Carmen LERENA Pelaez || Carmen Donata LERENA Beloqui   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, AND FIRST COUSINS ONCE REMOVED IF THE COLLA IDENTIFICATION EVER HOLDS. Mauricio LERENA (of Colla/Rosario, m. Casimira Montano 1837) || Mauricio LERENA (b. c.1836, of the First Section of Flores)   # *** REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: TWO MEN, AND THE WHOLE SEPARATION RESTS ON ONE WORD THAT WAS READ OFF THE PLATE. *** The Flores death act of May 1886 has a witness 'D. MAURICIO LERENA de CINCUENTA anos de edad, oriental, casado, hacendado', signing in his own hand. The AI transcript renders that age as 'VEINCUENTA', which is not a Spanish word - so the plate was opened, and it says CINCUENTA. *** FIFTY IN 1886 IS A BIRTH ABOUT 1836; THE OTHER MAURICIO MARRIED IN FEBRUARY 1837. *** *** THEY ARE NOT MERGED AND THEY ARE ALSO NOT DECLARED FATHER AND SON: *** 1836 falls BEFORE that marriage, so either the stated age is loose in the ordinary way - which ages on these acts routinely are - or the relationship is something else. *** AN UNRESOLVED RELATION IS NOT THE SAME AS AN UNEXAMINED ONE, and this one was examined at full resolution. *** THE TEST: the younger man's own marriage or death act, which would name his parents.
Dona Maria FERNANDEZ TRESGUERRAS (wife of Sancho) || Maria FERNANDEZ ALVAREZ (godmother, 1825)   # REVIEWED 9 Oct 2026: DIFFERENT WOMEN, AND THEY BELONG TO THE TWO LERENA HOUSES THIS ARCHIVE HAS PROVED SEPARATE. Maria FERNANDEZ TRESGUERRAS married SANCHO LLERENA at Canelones on 8 January 1791 and is the matriarch of the Montevideo trunk. Maria FERNANDEZ ALVAREZ was godmother at her own sister's wedding at San Jose on 16 September 1825, a daughter of Juan Fernandez Alvarez and Barbara Sotura, and belongs to RAMON's house. Thirty-four years, two departments, two compound surnames and two families that data/ramon-lerena-rosario.tsv showed do not meet. The gate matched on the commonest surname pairing in the River Plate plus the word 'godmother'. DECLARED BEFORE THE GATE ASKED TWICE: this row was written into the new person's own note as well, because a reader of the register should not have to open the pair file to learn that the resemblance was considered.

LERENA is known from TWO SIGNATURES on Durazno birth acts of 1915 at Tejera, where he signed for declarants who could not write - no age, no parents, no birthplace, and a middle initial written S. once and J. once. There is nothing to compare him on but the forename Pedro, which this register carries several times in two countries and three generations. NOT MERGED, AND NOT EXCLUDED EITHER: a man who exists only as a signature cannot be matched OR ruled out, and saying so is the honest state.
Eduardo LERENA (m. Maria Mercedes Razzello 1919) || Eduardo Avelino ZALDUANDO Lerena (m. Maria Ester Costa 1927)   # Different men. The second is the son of a Lerena MOTHER - Amelia or Angelica, daughter of Avelino Eduardo Lerena Traibel - born about 1901 and married at La Paz on 20 December 1927 aged 26. Different surname, different wife, different town, eight years apart. The gate pairs them only because "Lerena" is the trailing term in the second name. Both departments are Canelones, which is the one thing they share and is not an identity.

# A NOTE ON WHY TEN NEW PAIRS APPEARED AT ONCE ON 11 OCTOBER 2026, none of them a real duplicate.
# Maria Margarita Luisa ZALDUONDO Lerena's note was EXTENDED that day with the 1913 civil-index material,
# and tools/check-duplicates.py scores a pair partly on terms shared between the two rows' prose.
# Lengthening one row's note therefore manufactures overlap with every same-forename row in the
# register. *** THE GATE IS NOT WRONG TO ASK - it is doing what it was built to do - BUT THE COST OF
# WRITING A FULLER NOTE SHOULD NOT BE TEN DECLARATIONS. *** Recorded here rather than acted on,
# because changing the scorer to ignore prose would weaken the one signal that catches real
# duplicates whose names differ.
#
# 11 OCTOBER 2026: THE SURNAME IN THE DECLARATIONS ABOVE WAS WRONG, AND THEY HAVE BEEN REWRITTEN.
# Every "ZALSAMENDI" here was a misreading of ZALDUONDO, settled that day by reading both plates -
# the 1903 Pocitos baptism and the 1913 Montevideo death act - at full resolution. The pairs are
# still different people and the REASONS still hold; only the surname changed. See data/corrections.tsv.
# *** AND ONE PAIR IS GONE BECAUSE IT TURNED OUT TO BE ONE PERSON: *** "Amelia LERENA" was merged into
# ANGELICA MARIA LERENA VILLADEMOROS. The 1903 act calls her Amelia and the 1913 act calls her
# Angelica, and both attribute the SAME DAUGHTER to her - which is what forces one woman rather than
# two sisters. The merge is argued on her row, not here.
#
# 11 October 2026, second batch. MARIA ELENA ZALDUONDO LERENA is new to the register - a third
# child named on her father's 1913 death act and nothing else - and her forename collides with
# most of the Marias already here. She was born between about 1899 and 1912 (her brother Eduardo
# Avelino is b. c.1901 and her sister Maria Margarita Luisa 21 June 1903), at or near Montevideo,
# daughter of EDUARDO ZALDUONDO and ANGELICA MARIA LERENA VILLADEMOROS. Nothing below is a merge
# candidate; every one is a different father's line.

Maria Elena LERENA (m. 1906, mixed religion) || Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena   # EXCLUDED, and by a BLANK LINE ON A FORM. This pair was filed hours earlier as "not merged and not excluded either", because Maria Elena Zalduondo had no date at all and a woman with no date can be neither matched nor ruled out. Her parents' marriage act of 20 November 1897 then turned up and settled it: the act's "Legitiman" line is BLANK, so no child of theirs existed in November 1897 and all three were born after it. A girl born after November 1897 was not a bride in 1906. THE LESSON IS THAT AN UNDECIDABLE PAIR IS OFTEN ONLY UNDECIDABLE UNTIL THE RIGHT ACT IS READ, and that a form's EMPTY field can carry as much evidence as a filled one.
Maria Margarita Luisa ZALDUONDO Lerena || Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena   # SISTERS, not duplicates. Both are named on the same sentence of their father's death act of 22 August 1913 - "tres hijos llamados: MARIA ELENA, EDUARDO AVELINO y MARIA MARGARITA" - so the document itself asserts they are two people. Maria Margarita Luisa was baptised at Pocitos on 15 August 1903, born 21 June that year; Maria Elena has no date.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena   # Different women, though both descend from Avelino Lerena Fernandez - the first his daughter-line LERENA TRAIBEL, the second his great-granddaughter through Angelica. A common ancestor is not an identity.
Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena || Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena   # Different women. A Moratorio twin born 24 February 1880 against a Zalduondo child of about 1900. NOTE A COINCIDENCE THAT IS ONLY A COINCIDENCE: the Juez de Paz who wrote the 1913 Zalduondo death act was EDUARDO J. MORATORIO. Moratorio is not rare in Montevideo and a Juez de Paz signs whatever comes before him; no connection is claimed.
Maria de las Mercedes MORATORIO Lerena || Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena   # Different women. The other Moratorio twin of 24 February 1880; see above.
Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO) || Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena   # Different women. The first is the Trinidad Maria Lerena who married a Cardozo and is fixed by the 1911 dispensation diagram; the second is a Montevideo Zalduondo child.

# 11 OCTOBER 2026, from the finished Trinidad sweep. THREE OF THESE DESERVED THOUGHT AND GOT IT.
Aurora PELAEZ Lerena (confirmed, Trinidad 1891-1905) || Aurora LERENA Fernandez (m. Manuel VAQUERO)   # NOT MERGED, AND THIS PAIR IS THE UNCOMFORTABLE ONE OF THE BATCH: two women called Aurora, both at Trinidad, both in the register because of records of the 1890s. They are separated by SURNAME ORDER and by GENERATION. The first is a PELAEZ - daughter of a Pelaez father and a Lerena mother - known only from a confirmation list covering 1891 to 1905, so born somewhere between about 1877 and 1891. The second is a LERENA, born about 1868 per her children's registrations, already married to Manuel Vaquero and bearing children from 1891. A woman confirmed as a child in the 1890s is not a woman having children in the 1890s. NOT PROVED APART BY A DOCUMENT, only by the arithmetic of two index statements, and recorded that way.

# 11 OCTOBER 2026 - THE MANUEL LERENAS. There are at least three and they are separated by PARENTS,
# which is the only thing that separates men who share a forename and a surname in one small country.
Manuel LERENA (son of Mauricio, m. Jacinta Marina 1882) || Manuel LERENA Leonard (m. Juana Galarza 1890)   # DIFFERENT MEN, ON THEIR PARENTS AND ON NOTHING ELSE. The first is MANUEL [DOROTEO] LERENA MONTANO, born about 1856, son of MAURICIO LERENA and CASIMIRA MONTANO - the Colla couple married in 1837 - who married JACINTA MERINA SANTOS on 1 April 1882. The second is MANUEL LERENA LEONARD, born about 1866, son of PEDRO ANTONIO LERENA and TERESA LEONARD, who married JUANA GALARZA at the Cordon on 19 February 1890 and died at Montevideo on 1 April 1926. Both compounds carry their mothers' surnames - MONTANO against LEONARD - and a compound is not something an indexer invents. Ten years apart, different wives, different departments, different mothers.
Manuel LERENA (son of Mauricio, m. Jacinta Marina 1882) || Manuel Vicente LERENA Marino (m. 1910, Florida)   # FATHER AND SON, not duplicates. The 1910 marriage registration at Florida gives the boy's parents as "Manuel Doroteo" and "Jacinta" and surnames him LERENA MARINO - which is this man and his wife. A son carrying his father's forename is the commonest trap in this register and the reason this line exists.
Manuel LERENA Leonard (m. Juana Galarza 1890) || Manuel Vicente LERENA Marino (m. 1910, Florida)   # Different men, different houses. One is a son of Pedro Antonio Lerena and Teresa Leonard; the other of Manuel Doroteo Lerena and Jacinta Merina. The second surname of each compound settles it.

# 11 OCTOBER 2026 - FOUR FELICIA LERENAS, and the forename search that found them is the reason they
# are all here at once. Each is fixed by PARENTS or by a DATE, and no two can be the same woman.
Felicia LERENA (dau. of Mauricio, m. 19 Dec 1874 San Jose) || Felicia Clementina LERENA (m. 1886, Montevideo)   # Different women. The first is a daughter of MAURICIO LERENA and CASIMIRA MONTANO, married at San Jose de Mayo on 19 December 1874. The second is a daughter of MANUELA LEMOS, born about 1868 and married at Montevideo in 1886 - she was six years old when the first married. Different parents, different departments, twelve years apart.
Felicia LERENA (dau. of Mauricio, m. 19 Dec 1874 San Jose) || Felicia LERENA Ferreiroz (b. 6 May 1891)   # Different women, and seventeen years separate a bride from a newborn. The second was born 6 May 1891 at Montevideo to Manuel Lerena and Juana Ferreiroz.
Felicia LERENA (dau. of Mauricio, m. 19 Dec 1874 San Jose) || Felicia Leoncia LERENA (dau. of Bernabe and Florinda)   # Different women. The second is a daughter of BERNABE LERENA and FLORINDA CARDOZO, who married in 1888 - so she cannot be a bride of 1874. Different parents and a generation apart.
Felicia Clementina LERENA (m. 1886, Montevideo) || Felicia LERENA Ferreiroz (b. 6 May 1891)   # Different women. A bride of 1886 is not a child born in 1891. Different mothers - Manuela Lemos against Juana Ferreiroz - and the dates alone exclude it.
Felicia Clementina LERENA (m. 1886, Montevideo) || Felicia Leoncia LERENA (dau. of Bernabe and Florinda)   # Different women. A bride of 1886 at Montevideo, daughter of Manuela Lemos, against a daughter of a couple married at Trinidad in 1888 who herself married in 1931. Different parents, different departments, and the arithmetic forbids it.
Felicia LERENA Ferreiroz (b. 6 May 1891) || Felicia Leoncia LERENA (dau. of Bernabe and Florinda)   # NOT MERGED, AND THIS IS THE CLOSEST OF THE FOUR. Both are girls of the 1890s and both could plausibly marry in the 1930s. They are held apart by PARENTS: Manuel Lerena and Juana Ferreiroz at MONTEVIDEO against Bernabe Lerena and Florinda Cardozo at TRINIDAD, FLORES. Two different households in two different departments, and both parentages come from the records themselves rather than from inference. NOT PROVED APART BY A SINGLE DOCUMENT NAMING BOTH, which is why this is written down.
Juana Alejandra LERENA Echeverry || Juana de las Nieves LERENA (dau. of Olegaria)   # Different women. Juana Alejandra was christened 8 November 1873 at Trinidad, daughter of VITALINO LERENA and ANDREA ECHEVERRY. Juana de las Nieves is the daughter of OLEGARIA LERENA, baptised with her mother's surname and NO FATHER NAMED - which is the whole reason her mother is in this register. Different parents, and the second child's record is the only one either woman has.

# 11 OCTOBER 2026 - THE EMILIOS. Work-list row 137 had "Emilio Lerena" down as one unworked man.
# There are at least three, in two countries.
Emilio LERENA || Emilio Isidoro LERENA Recuero (b. 1906)   # Different men, and different continents. The first is a SPANISH immigrant from LOGRONO, born 1900, who arrived in Argentina on the Conte Rosso on 30 April 1930 and is known only from the CEMLA passenger list. The second was born at TRINIDAD, FLORES on 2 January 1906, son of Florencio P. Lerena Fernandez and Juana Recuero Santa Marta, and is a Uruguayan of a documented household. Six years apart and nothing else in common.
Emilio Isidoro LERENA Recuero (b. 1906) || Emilio LERENA (m. Paula CASARES)   # Different men, EXCLUDED BY ARITHMETIC. Emilio Isidoro was born 2 January 1906. Emilio x Paula Casares has a son, EMILIO DELFINO LERENA, with a marriage registration at Flores in 1931 - a man born in 1906 cannot have a son marrying at twenty-five. The elder Emilio is therefore a generation above, which is consistent with row 137 having met him as a FATHER in a sweep of the interior. NEITHER MAN'S BIRTH IS ON A CARD THAT CARRIES DATES, so this rests on the son's marriage year and is stated as such.
Dr. D. Damaso LARRANAGA (Vicar, 1833) || Dr. D. Joaquin REQUENA (witness, 1871)   # Different men, and the gate is pairing them on the honorific "Dr. D." rather than on a name. Damaso Larranaga was Vicar at Montevideo in 1833; Joaquin Requena was a witness at a Cordon wedding in December 1871, thirty-eight years later. No shared forename or surname at all.
Dr. D. Juan Ciriaco OTAEGUI (priest, 1833) || Dr. D. Joaquin REQUENA (witness, 1871)   # Different men, paired on the honorific alone. A priest of 1833 against a wedding witness of 1871, with no name in common.
Juana Alejandra LERENA Echeverry || Juana LERENA (b. 25 June 1912, Buenos Aires)   # Different women, and different countries. Juana Alejandra was christened at Trinidad, Flores, URUGUAY on 8 November 1873, daughter of Vitalino Lerena and Andrea Echeverry. Juana was born at BUENOS AIRES on 25 June 1912 to two Spanish-born Lerenas, Eloy and Felisa. Thirty-nine years and a river apart.
Juana de las Nieves LERENA (dau. of Olegaria) || Juana LERENA (b. 25 June 1912, Buenos Aires)   # Different women. The first is a Uruguayan child baptised with her mother's surname because no father was named on the act; the second is the ARGENTINE-born daughter of a named married couple, Eloy and Felisa Lerena, both naturales de Espana. Different country, different century of record, and the second has two parents on her act where the first has one.

Vicente Antonio LARENA || Vicente LERENA DIAZ (Registro Civil, Pueblo Brugo 1938)   # NOT MERGED, AND NOT EXCLUDED EITHER - and this is the most uncomfortable pair of 11 October 2026, because BOTH MEN ARE IN ENTRE RIOS. Vicente Antonio LARENA is at GUALEGUAYCHU, married Vicenta Mercedes Fuentes, with children born 1906 and 1913 - so born about 1875-1885. Vicente LERENA DIAZ keeps the civil register at PUEBLO BRUGO, departamento de PARANA, in 1938 - and a man born about 1880 would be about fifty-eight then, which is an ordinary age for that office. DIFFERENT DEPARTMENTS OF ONE PROVINCE IS NOT A SEPARATION: Gualeguaychu and Parana are a day apart. AND THE SPELLING IS NOT A SEPARATION EITHER - this archive has catalogued LERENA read as LARENA, SERENA, LORENA, LESINA and six more, and keeps a false-zero row about exactly that. WHAT WOULD DECIDE IT: Vicente Larena has no mother on record and Vicente Lerena Diaz has no children on record, so the two facts each man carries cannot be set against each other. The test is an act naming either man's parents - the Gualeguaychu marriage of Vicente Antonio Larena and Vicenta Mercedes Fuentes, or any Lerena Diaz entry in the Parana registers. BEARS DIRECTLY ON WORK-LIST ROW 159, which asks for Entre Rios to be mapped and now has three reasons rather than one.
Juan Jose LERENA || Juan Jose LERENA y LERENA (of Navarrete, Logrono; Buenos Aires 1900)   # NOT MERGED, AND THE STRONGEST UNMERGED PAIR IN THIS FILE. Juan Jose LERENA: b.1876, birthplace given as LOGRONO, Spanish, soltero, 'comercio', landed at Buenos Aires 1920, 1928, 1929 (CEMLA). Juan Jose LERENA y LERENA: 'mayor de edad, soltero, dependiente, natural de NAVARRETE, provincia de LOGRONO, vecino de esta Capital', consular registration 951, Buenos Aires, 14 November 1900. Same forename pair, same province, same civil state, same trade, same city, and 1876 makes him 24 that November. KEPT APART BECAUSE CEMLA GIVES THE PROVINCE AND NOT THE VILLAGE, and because a merge would import a whole named household - a mother, two brothers, a village property - onto an index row carrying nothing but a surname and three sailings. What would settle it: the Navarrete parish registers, or any later Buenos Aires act naming him with both surnames.

Maria LERENA || Maria Adelina LERENA LARRIERA   # NOT THE SAME WOMAN, and this is the one pair in this file where that matters. Maria LERENA is the mother named on Pablo Armando's 1908 enrolment card - no birth date, no birthplace, no act of her own, the woman this archive exists to find. Maria Adelina LERENA LARRIERA was born in the Departamento de San Jose on 6 July 1879 and christened at the Montevideo cathedral on 13 October 1879, daughter of Pedro Alberto Ysabelino Lerena Traibel and Maria Ines Larriera. SHE WAS TWO AND A HALF YEARS OLD WHEN PABLO ARMANDO WAS BORN IN FEBRUARY 1882, so she cannot be his mother, and no other reasoning is needed. Recorded because the gate pairs them on forename and surname and will go on doing so, and because a reader who meets a documented "Maria Lerena" in this register deserves to be told at once that she is not the one.

She would be 28 in 1891 and could be this godmother, but the act gives no second surname and nothing ties them. THE FORENAME MERCEDES RUNS THROUGH THIS FAMILY TOO HARD TO SEPARATE ANYONE, and the Cordon act gives its godmother no age, no parents and no second surname. What would settle it: the act itself off the plate, which may carry more than the names panel does.

Eduardo LERENA || Eduardo LERENA (m. Maria Mercedes Razzello 1919)   # NOT MERGED, AND THE ARITHMETIC IS UNCOMFORTABLY GOOD. Eduardo LERENA: named as the father on the baptism of ANA MARIA, born 12 January 1921 and baptised 19 January 1921 at Nuestra Senora de los Desamparados, partido de Adolfo Alsina, Buenos Aires province; the act calls her 'hija legitima' and gives the mother as MARIA MERCEDES RAZZETTI, read off the plate. Eduardo LERENA (m. Maria Mercedes Razzello 1919): dispensed the conciliar banns at Montevideo on 22 December 1919 to marry 'Dona MARIA MERCEDES RAZZELLO, feligresa de LAS PIEDRAS', read from a machine transcript. SAME FORENAME, SAME WIFE'S FORENAMES, AND RAZZELLO vs RAZZETTI IS ONE HAND READ TWICE - both are far closer to each other than either is to the index's 'Rozzetti'. THIRTEEN MONTHS BETWEEN THE DISPENSATION AND THE CHILD. KEPT APART because no act yet read says the Buenos Aires man came from Las Piedras, and because a merge here would carry a Uruguayan origin onto an Argentine household on the strength of a date and two forenames. What would settle it: the Montevideo marriage act behind the 1919 dispensation, which will name his parents, or any Argentine act giving him as 'oriental'.
Ana Rosario LERENA Traibel || Ana Maria LERENA Razzetti   # Different people, seventy years and a river apart. Ana Rosario LERENA Traibel: daughter of Avelino Lerena and Ambrosia Traibel, baptised at San Francisco de Asis, Montevideo, 12 March 1858. Ana Maria LERENA Razzetti: born 12 January 1921 in the partido de Adolfo Alsina, Buenos Aires province, daughter of Eduardo Lerena and Maria Mercedes Razzetti, baptised at Nuestra Senora de los Desamparados on 19 January 1921 - plate read. They share only the forename Ana and the surname.
Eduardo LERENA || Eduardo Avelino ZALDUANDO Lerena (m. Maria Ester Costa 1927)   # Different men, and the second is not a Lerena by his father. Eduardo LERENA: father of Ana Maria Lerena Razzetti, in the partido de Adolfo Alsina, Buenos Aires province, January 1921. Eduardo Avelino ZALDUANDO Lerena: a ZALDUANDO whose mother was a Lerena, married Maria Ester Costa in 1927 at Montevideo. The surname is in a different position in each name, which is the whole distinction.
MARIA MERCEDES (daughter of Juana, enslaved) || Maria Mercedes RAZZETTI (wife of Eduardo LERENA)   # Different women, a century and a condition apart. MARIA MERCEDES: baptised at the Montevideo Catedral on 26 September 1812, daughter of JUANA, an enslaved woman in Sancho Llerena's household, and given no surname of her own because the act gives her none. Maria Mercedes RAZZETTI: an Italian-Argentine wife and mother in the partido de Adolfo Alsina in 1921. The pairing is an artefact of a very common pair of forenames, and the first woman's row carries no surname at all.

Arturo LERENA GARCIA (m. Amalia Diaz) || Arturo LERENA (oriental, b. c.1857, father of Eduardo)   # DIFFERENT MEN, SEPARATED BY NATIONALITY ON THE FACE OF TWO ACTS. Arturo LERENA GARCIA: SPANISH, a journalist, married Amalia Diaz, sons surnamed LERENA DIAZ - one dead at Valparaiso in 1894, one married at Crespo, Entre Rios, in 1924. Arturo LERENA (oriental): 'de nacionalidad ORIENTAL, de estado casado, de sesenta y dos anos, domiciliado en MONTEVIDEO' on his son's marriage act of 11 January 1920, so born about 1857; wife EMA REGULES; son EDUARDO born at Montevideo on 22 April 1886. A Spaniard and a Uruguayan of the same forename and the same generation. NOT EXCLUDED FOREVER - a man can be recorded under either nationality by a careless clerk - but nothing joins them and two documents say different things.
Ema REGULES (wife of Arturo LERENA) || Ema LERENA (godmother, Cordon 1900-02)   # A CANDIDACY, NOT A MERGE. Ema REGULES: wife of Arturo Lerena, born about 1864, named on her son's marriage act of 1920. Ema LERENA: 'Ema. Lerena', godmother at the Cordon between August 1900 and October 1902, beside an EDUARDO LERENA. A married woman appears in a parish book under her husband's surname, and Ema Regules would have been about thirty-six with a son Eduardo aged fourteen to sixteen - which is the weakness, because fourteen is young for a godfather. This register also holds EMA SIXTA LERENA JUANICO and MARIA EMA LERENA LENGUAS. What would settle it: the Cordon act off the plate, which may give the godparents' residence.

Eduardo LERENA (b.1886, m. Maria Mercedes RAZZETTI 1920) || Eduardo LERENA (godfather, Cordon 1900-02)   # NOT MERGED, AND THIS ONE IS GENUINELY OPEN. The first was born at Montevideo on 22 APRIL 1886 and married Maria Mercedes Razzetti at Santa Lucia in January 1920; his mother is EMA REGULES. The second stood godfather at the Cordon between August 1900 and October 1902 beside an 'Ema. Lerena'. IF THEY ARE ONE MAN, THE CORDON PAIR IS A MOTHER AND HER SON - Ema Regules at about thirty-six and Eduardo at FOURTEEN TO SIXTEEN. That is the attraction and also the objection: fourteen is young to stand godfather, though not impossible for a brother or a nephew of the family. NOTHING ELSE JOINS THEM - the Cordon act gives no ages, no residence and no second surname, and this register holds four men of the name. What would settle it: the Cordon act off the plate.
Juan Arturo LERENA Traibel || Arturo LERENA (oriental, b. c.1857, father of Eduardo)   # NOT MERGED, AND THE OBJECTION IS THREE YEARS. Juan Arturo LERENA Traibel: born 16 February 1861, christened at the Cordon on 31 December 1861, son of Avelino Lerena and Ambrosia Traibel. Arturo LERENA (oriental): 'el DOCTOR DON ARTURO LERENA, natural de esta Republica, soltero, de VEINTE Y DOS ANOS de edad, hijo legitimo de Don AVELINO LERENA y Dona AMBROSIA TRAIBEL', married at the Catedral de Montevideo on 23 June 1880; his son's act of 1920 gives him as sixty-two, so born about 1857-58. SAME PARENTS, SAME CITY, SAME GENERATION, AND THE FORENAME IS THERE IN BOTH. A man born in February 1861 was NINETEEN in June 1880, not twenty-two, and the archive holds his baptismal date rather than an estimate. An age on a marriage act bends by a year; it does not usually bend by three, and TWO INDEPENDENT DOCUMENTS - the 1880 marriage and the 1920 marriage of his son - agree on 1857-58. The couple already have eleven known children, so a twelfth is no strain. What would settle it: Arturo's own baptism at the Cordon or the Catedral about 1857-58, or any act naming Juan Arturo as a doctor.
