Dr Jorge CALVETTE (physician, Montevideo 1936) || Dr Ambrosio LERENA (witness, 1880)   # Not the same man and not the same kind of doctor, recorded because this archive spent a day learning the difference. Calvette signed the medical certificate for Arturo Lerena's death in 1936 and is certainly a physician. Ambrosio Lerena was styled Doctor as a witness at a wedding in 1880, in a country where that title belongs to lawyers too, and nothing says which he was.
Florencio LERENA (of Trinidad) || Florencio SERENA (of Cerro Largo, d. before 1938)   # CLOSED BY A PLACE, 12 October 2026. This pair asked whether the Trinidad Florencio had a second wife called Teresa Lerena. HE DID NOT: the 1938 Montevideo marriage act of the daughter puts that family in the DEPARTAMENTO DE CERRO LARGO - the mother domiciled there, the daughter born there in 1908 - four departments from Trinidad, and names the father FLORENCIO SERENA, fallecido. The Trinidad Florencio married Dona Juana Recuero. DIFFERENT MEN, and the 'three readings are open' note this entry used to carry is superseded.
Teresa LLERENA || Teresa SERENA (widow, of Cerro Largo)   # Not merged. Teresa LLERENA is in this register under the double-L spelling from a different source; Teresa LERENA is known only as the mother of Victorina in one index entry. Spelling is no separation in this archive and no join either, and neither row carries a date, a place or an event to compare.
Alejandro LERENA (the elder, m. Margarita Fernandez Alvarez) || Don Alejandro LERENA (of San Jose de Mayo, 1846)   # REWRITTEN 12 October 2026, BECAUSE THE QUESTION CHANGED. This pair used to ask whether the elder Alejandro had a second wife called Barbara Lerena. HE DID NOT: the plate of the 1846 burial says 'hijo natural de BARBARA, ESCLAVA DE D.n ALEJANDRO LERENA', and the marriage was an indexer's reading. What is left is a DON ALEJANDRO LERENA at SAN JOSE DE MAYO in April 1846 who held a woman in slavery. The elder Alejandro married at San Jose on 16 September 1825 and was at Trinidad by 1855 - the right town, the right decade, the right style of address, and no wife, age or parents on the 1846 act to test it with. NOT MERGED. What would settle it: any San Jose act of the 1840s naming Alejandro Lerena with Margarita Fernandez Alvarez, or a padron of the household.

Maria LERENA || Maria SERENA/LERENA (m. Joaquin Costales 1865)   # NOT EXCLUDED ON EVIDENCE, AND NOT A CANDIDATE EITHER - written by hand on 12 October 2026 because the gate did not pair them and should have. Maria LERENA is the mother named on Pablo Armando's 1908 enrolment card, with no birth, no place and no act of her own. Maria SERENA/LERENA (m. Joaquin Costales 1865) is a woman the record index returns as the daughter of ANTONIO LERENA and SABINA LERENA, married at San Francisco de Asis, Montevideo, on 25 May 1865. SHE IS THE CLOSEST THING THE INDEX HAS PRODUCED TO THE CONFIGURATION ON THE CARD: a Maria Lerena who is herself the child of two Lerenas. THE ARITHMETIC DOES NOT FORBID IT - a woman marrying in 1865 at about twenty was about thirty-seven in February 1882 - BUT IT REQUIRES SOMETHING NO RECORD SHOWS: that she was widowed or left by Joaquin Costales and then partnered with a JUAN CARLOS LERENA, in Argentina, by 1881. Nothing says so. She is recorded here so that the next reader who meets her does not have to do this arithmetic again, and so that nobody quietly promotes her. What would settle it either way: her own act, or a death or remarriage for Joaquin Costales.

Carlos A. LERENA (plaintiff, Buenos Aires 1890) || Dr LLERENA (curador, Buenos Aires courts 1889)   # NOT MERGED. Carlos A. LERENA sued the estate of Don Carlos Maria Escalada for a broker's commission in February 1890 - a PARTY, and the defence says he produced no document. Dr LLERENA was appointed CURADOR in the Feliciano Aguirre probate in 1889 - an OFFICER of the same court, styled Doctor, which in Argentina as in Uruguay is the style of an abogado. A man can be both a lawyer and a litigant, so this is not an exclusion; but nothing joins them except a surname and a city in the same decade, and the 1889 act gives the curador no forename at all. What would settle it: a forename for the curador, in any of the four probates that name him.

Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885) || Maria LERENA Pelaez (b.1866, dau. of Bernabe)   # NOT MERGED, AND THE ROW ITSELF SAYS THE MERGE IS LIKELIER THAN THE SPLIT. Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885) is given as born about 1869; Maria LERENA Pelaez was born 13 May 1866 and christened at Trinidad on the 25th, a daughter of Bernabe Lerena and Casimira Pelaez, found under the LLERENA spelling on 12 October 2026. THREE YEARS APART, ONE TOWN, ONE SET OF PARENTS - and the 1869 is itself an estimate from an age, not a date from an act. A woman who married a Maximo PELAEZ in 1885 and whose mother was a Casimira PELAEZ is also exactly the shape of the Lerena-Pelaez knot this archive has documented five times over. WHAT WOULD SETTLE IT: the 1885 marriage act, which will give her age and her parents. Until then two rows, because merging on a estimated year would be the same move this archive spent 12 October undoing.

Maria LERENA Pelaez was christened at Trinidad on 25 May 1866, also a daughter of Bernabe and Casimira. SAME PARENTS, SO THEY ARE EITHER SISTERS OR ONE WOMAN - and a household that already contains a Maria is exactly where a reused name appears, which this register has met before. The 1889 act will give the bride's age; nothing else will do it.

Maria Lerena || Maria LARENA (arr. 1911)   # NOT THE SAME WOMAN, and the first is not a person this archive can point at. Maria LARENA (arr. 1911) is a CEMLA passenger on the ship BARCELONA, arriving in Argentina in 1911, held here as an UNPLACED namesake. Emilia Alberta LERENA Bassani || Emilia LERENA Vega (b. c.1832)   # Different women, a generation or two apart. Emilia LERENA Vega is a daughter of Pedro Antonio LERENA and Vicenta VEGA, entered at eighteen in Montevideo household 229 - an enumeration whose date is CONTESTED (the volume says 1850, this archive says c.1833), so her birth year is an estimate resting on an assumption. Emilia Alberta LERENA Bassani carries a compound surname of record from a later, placed household. Manuela LERENA (mother of Nicolas, 1869) || Manuela LERENA (b. in AFRICA; Sarandi 229, 1850)   # Different women, and the padron decides it. The Manuela of Sarandi 229 is SIXTY in 1850, so born about 1790, and the padron gives her patria as AFRICA; she carries the surname by ditto in a house where the only white entry is an eleven-year-old girl. The Manuela who is mother of Nicolas bore him in 1869, when the Sarandi woman would have been seventy-nine. Seventy-nine years and a different continent of birth.
Amelia LERENA Cardozo (m. Abel Conto 1920) || Amelia LERENA (d. 11 November 1871, aged 9)   # Different people, half a century apart. Amelia LERENA Cardozo married Abel CONTO in 1920 and carries a compound surname of record. The Amelia of 1871 died at NINE YEARS OLD on 11 November 1871 at the Cordon, so born about 1862; she is index-level, from a poor machine transcript that runs the margin into the body, and the plate has not been opened. A child dead in 1871 does not marry in 1920.
Xavier LAVINA (the younger, m. Leonarda LERENA) || Don Xavier LAVINA (the elder, m. Manuela GOYENECHE)   # FATHER AND SON, and the act says so in one word: the death entry of 5 August 1869 calls the father of the two dead girls "D. Xavier (hijo)" - the younger - which is only written when an elder of the same name exists. The elder is named in his son Emilio Lavina y Goyeneche s marriage dispensation of 1861 as husband of Dona Manuela Goyeneche. Two rows on purpose, the same way Pedro LERENA Vega and Pedro A. LERENA the younger are two rows.
Jose LLERENA (arr. 1910) || Jose LLERENA (arr. 1926)   # NOT MERGED, AND NOT SETTLED. Two Buenos Aires passenger entries with the same birth year, 1887: a Spanish day-labourer arriving from Bilbao on the Sinai in October 1910, and a Spanish carpenter born at Logrono arriving on the Infanta Isabel de Borbon in November 1926. One man returning after sixteen years is possible; two men of the same age is possible too. Neither entry names a parent or a place of birth on both sides. Kept apart until a passenger note or an act ties them. (Raised by the 13 October cloud audit as a low-confidence duplicate.)
Enrique LERENA Juanico || Enrique Carlos LERENA (clase 1899, Distrito Militar 3)   # Different men twenty-six years apart: Enrique Lerena Juanico was born about 1873 and died at Montevideo in May 1914, aged forty-one; the other is a conscript of the clase of 1899 in an Argentine military district. The shared words are the burial and civil acts both of Enrique's records cite.

They share a forename, a parish and a type of act, nothing else.

Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885) || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # NOT MERGED. Different women on the record: her husband is Maximo Pelaez, married 1885 at Trinidad; the 1920 Maria's husband is Jose Maria Reyes at the Cordon. Nothing joins them.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. Adelina was the widow of Carlos Alberto Fein from May 1919 and her death registration of 1946 still calls her his wife (de Fein); nothing shows she married again, and the 1920 entry says only 'Maria Lerena'. Different names on the record, and an untested possibility that a second marriage was missed.
Maria Elena LERENA (m. 1906, mixed religion) || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # NOT MERGED. The 1906 Maria Elena was dispensed a mixed-religion impediment to marry a Don Guillermo; the 1920 Maria is married to Jose Maria Reyes, with no forename beyond Maria. Different husbands on the record, and a possible second marriage untested.
Maria Adela LERENA Carniglia || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # NOT MERGED. Maria Adela was born at Mendoza in 1899 to Carlos Lerena and Adela Carniglia; the 1920 Maria is a wife of Jose Maria Reyes at the Cordon, a man of seventy-seven. Different forename, different country of birth.
Maria Mercedes LERENA Carniglia || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # NOT MERGED. Maria Mercedes was born at Mendoza in 1902 to Carlos Lerena and Adela Carniglia; the 1920 Maria is the wife of a man of seventy-seven buried at the Cordon. Different forename, different country.
Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # NOT MERGED. Maria Elena carries the ZALDUONDO compound and is named as a child of Angelica Lerena on her father's death act of 1913; the 1920 Maria carries no compound and a husband named Reyes. Different forename and different surname of record.
Maria Mercedes LERENA (godmother, Cordon 1891-92) || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. Both appear at the Cordon, but the godmother of 1891-92 is entered as Maria Mercedes and the 1920 wife as Maria; nothing gives either a husband or a birth, so they are neither joined nor ruled out.
Maria SERENA/LERENA (m. Joaquin Costales 1865) || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. The 1865 Maria married Joaquin Costales at San Francisco de Asis and is the daughter of Antonio and Sabina Lerena; the 1920 Maria is married to Jose Maria Reyes. A widow of Costales who remarried is possible and nothing says it happened.
Maria Dolores MORATORIO Lerena || Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920)   # NOT MERGED. Maria Dolores carries the MORATORIO compound and is placed by her grandparents; the 1920 Maria is named only as the wife of Jose Maria Reyes. Different forename and surname of record.
Sara Esmeralda LERENA Fernandez || Sara LERENA (d. 18 August 1892, aged 18)   # Different people, eight years apart. Sara Esmeralda was born at Trinidad on 3 November 1900 to Pedro Segundo Lerena and Petrona Fernandez; the Sara of the Cordon was buried on 18 August 1892, aged eighteen, and cannot have been born in 1900.
Sara SERENA (godmother, Trinidad 1905-09) || Sara LERENA (d. 18 August 1892, aged 18)   # Different people. The Sara of Trinidad stood godmother between 1905 and 1909; the Sara of the Cordon was buried on 18 August 1892. A woman buried in 1892 does not stand at a font in 1905.
Teresa SERENA (widow, of Cerro Largo) || Teresa LERENA (mother of Sara, 1892)   # NOT MERGED. The Teresa of Cerro Largo is a widow named on her daughter's marriage act of January 1938, her surname perhaps her husband Florencio's; the Teresa of the Cordon was the mother of an eighteen-year-old in 1892, which would make the 1938 widow about ninety. Different department, forty-six years apart, nothing joins them.
Benito LERENA || Benito LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885)   # NOT MERGED. Benito Lerena is a day-labourer born about 1854 at Artigas on his own civil record; the Larranaga child is named only, in a list of 1885, with no age, and carries his father's surname first. Nothing joins them.
Carmen LERENA Pelaez || Carmen LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885)   # NOT MERGED. Carmen Lerena Pelaez was born about 1877 at Trinidad, Flores, to Maximo Pelaez's household; the Larranaga child is named only at Montevideo in 1885 and her parents are Benito Larranaga and Martina Lerena.
Carmen LERENA (d. 1841, aged three) || Carmen LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885)   # Different people forty-four years apart: the Carmen of 1841 died at three and was the daughter of Justo Lerena and Juana Quintana; the Larranaga child is listed as a living child in March 1885.
Carmen Donata LERENA Beloqui || Carmen LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885)   # NOT MERGED. Carmen Donata was born at Sarandi, Flores, on 17 February 1895, a decade after the Larranaga children were listed on their father's death act, and carries a Beloqui compound.
Doroteo Donato LERENA Beloqui || Doroteo LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885)   # NOT MERGED. Doroteo Donato was born at Trinidad, Flores, on 12 December 1875 to a Beloqui mother, ONE man carrying both forenames; the Larranaga list names Donato and Doroteo as TWO children of a Montevideo household. The shared forenames are noted as a coincidence and nothing more.
Doroteo LERENA (m. Isidora Cardozo) || Doroteo LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. Both are Doroteos with a Lerena in the name, and a son of Martina Lerena might have used his mother's surname; but nothing gives either an age or a place that would join them, and the Larranaga child is entered under his father's surname.
Estefania LERENA (m. Servando GONZALEZ) || Estefania GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # MOTHER AND DAUGHTER, TWO WOMEN. The 1892 civil act of Servando Tomas Gonzalez names his wife Estefania Lerena and, among their nine children, an Estefania - the daughter named for her mother. Both rows cite the same index record because the act names both. The mother was born about 1832; the daughter is a child of that marriage.
Carmen LERENA Pelaez || Carmen GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. Carmen Lerena Pelaez was born about 1877 at Trinidad to Bernabe Lerena and Casimira Pelaez; the Carmen of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena. Different named parents.
Carmen LERENA (d. 1841, aged three) || Carmen GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # Different people fifty years apart: the Carmen of 1841 died at three, the daughter of Justo Lerena and Juana Quintana; the Carmen of 1892 is a living child listed on her father's death act.
Carmen Donata LERENA Beloqui || Carmen GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. Carmen Donata was born on 17 February 1895 at Sarandi, Flores, after the 1892 act had already listed a Carmen among the Gonzalez children; she carries the Beloqui compound.
Carmen LARRANAGA Lerena (child, named 1885) || Carmen GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. The Larranaga Carmen is a daughter of Benito Larranaga and Martina Lerena, listed on her father's death act of 1885; the Gonzalez Carmen is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena, listed on his act of 1892. Different parents, different acts.
Francisco LERENA (b.1890, 1895 census) || Francisco GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. Francisco Lerena was born in 1890; the Francisco of the Gonzalez household is a child of Estefania Lerena, who was about fifty-eight in 1890, and he is listed as a child alongside siblings born from 1854.
Maria Julia Margarita Josefa Emirena Justiniana LERENA Juanico (m. HERRERA) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She is a daughter of Luis Lerena Lenguas and Julia Juanico, married to a Herrera; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Carlota LERENA Salvanach || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the Salvanach compound and a documented birth; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Adelina LERENA LARRIERA || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She was born on 6 July 1879 at San Jose, a daughter of Alberto Lerena and Maria Ines Larriera; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Consuelo LERENA LARRIERA || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the LARRIERA compound, a daughter of Alberto Lerena and Maria Ines Larriera's household; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Antonieta LERENA LARRIERA || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the LARRIERA compound, a daughter of Alberto Lerena and Maria Ines Larriera's household; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Ines LERENA LARRIERA (de GARDONE) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the LARRIERA compound and married a Gardone; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Adelina LERENA Traibel (de FEIN) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She was born on 13 August 1865 to Avelino Lerena and Ambrosia Traibel and married Carlos Alberto Fein; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Carlota MORATORIO Lerena || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the MORATORIO compound, a daughter of Fernando Moratorio and Josefa Lerena Traibel, christened at the Catedral in 1880; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria de las Mercedes MORATORIO Lerena || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the MORATORIO compound, a daughter of Fernando Moratorio and Josefa Lerena Traibel, christened at the Catedral in 1880; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Dolores MORATORIO Lerena || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the MORATORIO compound and is placed by her grandparents; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria LERENA (m. Eliseo CARDOZO) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. Her husband is Eliseo Cardozo, named on a dispensation; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Margarita Luisa ZALDUONDO Lerena || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the ZALDUONDO compound, a daughter of Angelica Lerena; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Elena ZALDUONDO Lerena || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She carries the ZALDUONDO compound, a daughter of Angelica Lerena named on her father's death act of 1913; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria LERENA (of Trinidad, m. Maximo Pelaez 1885) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. Her husband is Maximo Pelaez, married 1885 at Trinidad; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena, whose family also lived at Trinidad - which is a reason to look at it, not a tie. Untested whether the two are one woman.
Maria Elena LERENA (m. 1906, mixed religion) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. The 1906 Maria Elena was dispensed a mixed-religion impediment to marry a Don Guillermo; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena. Different forename.
Maria Mercedes LERENA Carniglia || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She was born at Mendoza in 1902 to Carlos Lerena and Adela Carniglia; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria Mercedes LERENA (godmother, Cordon 1891-92) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. The godmother stood at the Cordon in 1891-92 and is entered as Maria Mercedes Lerena; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena, whose father was buried at the Cordon in 1892. Nothing gives either a birth or a husband that would join or part them.
Maria SERENA/LERENA (m. Joaquin Costales 1865) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # NOT MERGED. She married Joaquin Costales at San Francisco de Asis in 1865, a daughter of Antonio and Sabina Lerena; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena.
Maria LERENA (m. Jose Maria REYES, Cordon 1920) || Maria GONZALEZ Lerena (named 1892)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. The wife of Jose Maria Reyes is entered at the Cordon in 1920 only as 'Maria Lerena'; the Maria of the 1892 act is a daughter of Servando Gonzalez and Estefania Lerena, a Montevideo family, and could have been entered by her mother's surname. Nothing gives either a birth, a parent or a place that would join or part them.
Pedro LLERENA || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # NOT MERGED. Pedro Llerena is married to Sofia Clavijo and fathers children from 1884; nothing names him with Teresa Leonar, whose act leaves one son, Manuel.
Pedro Antonio LERENA (son of Sancho) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED, AS A POSSIBLE FATHER. Pedro Antonio, son of Sancho (born 1793), married Vicenta Vega. Teresa Leonar's husband is the son of 'Pedro y Maria Pereyra' on the 1864 christening of his daughter; that 'Pedro' is unidentified and would be Pedro Antonio only after a second marriage to a Maria Pereyra, which nothing records. They are not the same man: the husband was born after about 1835.
Pedro Alberto Ysabelino LERENA Traibel (the ALBERTO of 1852-1921) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. Pedro Alberto died on 2 March 1921 and was married to Maria Ines Larriera; Teresa Leonar's husband was dead by 27 August 1901.
Pedro LORENA (father of Victor, d. before 1889) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # NOT MERGED. Pedro Lorena's wife is Josefa Segueira and his parents Fidel Lerena and Maria Ruedas; nothing names him with Teresa Leonar, whose marriage left one son, Manuel.
Pedro LERENA Vega (b. 1828) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # DIFFERENT MEN, SETTLED 17 October 2026. The 1864 christening of Manuela (plate read) names Teresa Leonar's husband, Pedro Lerena, as the son of Pedro and Maria Pereyra; Pedro Lerena Vega's mother was Vicenta Vega. Pedro Vega died in November 1896 with a wife Manuela Lemos; Teresa's husband was dead by August 1901 with a wife Teresa.
Pedro Segundo LERENA Villademoros || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. Pedro Segundo of Trinidad is married to Petrona Fernandez and fathers children registered 1898-1903, so he was alive after Teresa Leonar's husband was dead.
Pedro Gualberto LERENA Villademoros || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. Pedro Gualberto of Trinidad is married to Rosaura Alvarez and has children christened in 1901 and 1902; Teresa Leonar's husband was a Montevideo man dead by August 1901.
Pedro S. LERENA (of Tejera, Durazno, 1915) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. Pedro S. was alive and signing birth acts at Tejera, Durazno, in 1915; Teresa Leonar's husband was dead by 1901.
Pedro Ricardo LERENA Fernandez || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. Pedro Ricardo was born at Trinidad on 27 May 1903, after Teresa Leonar's husband was dead.
Pedro LERENA Arrascaeta (b. and d. 1911) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. Pedro Lerena Arrascaeta was born and died in 1911.
Pedro PERENA (m. Angela Francisca Montesdeoca) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # NOT MERGED. A Trinidad household with a wife Angela Francisca Montesdeoca; nothing names him with Teresa Leonar.
Pedro LERENA || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men. This Pedro was born about 1842 in Italy, a farmer of Jesus Maria, San Jose, married to Juana Camacho and alive in 1910; Teresa Leonar's husband was an oriental dead by 1901.
Pedro A. LERENA (the younger, declarant 1896) || Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901)   # Different men by generation. Pedro A. the younger is a son of Pedro LERENA Vega (born about 1834), so he cannot have fathered Teresa Leonar's son Manuel, born about 1863-66; he was also alive and declaring his father's death in 1896.
Manuela LERENA (mother of Nicolas, 1869) || Manuela LERENA Leonar (b. 9 April 1864)   # Different women. The Manuela of Trinidad is the mother of a child christened in 1869; the Manuela born on 9 April 1864 would have been five.
Manuela LERENA (b. in AFRICA; Sarandi 229, 1850) || Manuela LERENA Leonar (b. 9 April 1864)   # Different women seventy-four years apart: the Manuela of Sarandi 229 was sixty in 1850 and born in Africa; the Manuela of 1864 is a baby baptised at the Catedral to Pedro Lerena and Teresa Leonar.

Amelia LERENA (d. 11 November 1871, aged 9) || Amelia LERENA Morales (b. 1862, Cordon)   # LIKELY THE SAME GIRL, NOT MERGED. A girl 'Amelia Lerena, oriental, de nueve anos' died at the Cordon on 11 November 1871 (entry 619, cura Ojeda, who also signed the 1863 christening); the Amelia born on 2 May 1862 would have been nine and a half, in the same parish. Neither act names the other's parents - the death entry names none - so the two rows stay apart until something joins them.

Pedro Antonio LERENA (husband of Teresa Leonar, dead by 1901) || Pedro A. LERENA (m. Trinidad Morales, Cordon 1863)   # KEPT APART - PROBABLY TWO MEN, NOT PROVED. Teresa's husband was dispensed two banns for his marriage to Teresa Leonard on 8 June 1861 and was her husband at their daughter's christening in 1864; Trinidad Morales is the wife of the Pedro A. who baptised Amelia at the Cordon on 8 February 1863, between those two dates. One man with two wives at once is the only way they are one, which nothing suggests.

Manuela MUNIZ (m. Juan Nepomuceno Gonzalez) || Manuela MUNIZ (wife of Pedro Vaquero)   # [26 October 2026: TWO SISTERS MARRIED THE TWO SONS - Estefania Lerena married Servando Gonzalez (mother Manuela Muniz) on 13 July 1853 and Natalia Lerena married Manuel Vaquero (mother Manuela Muniz) on 5 August 1861, Servando being about seven years the elder. Two men whose mothers share a name, marrying sisters, makes ONE WOMAN - a widow who married twice - likelier than two, but nothing says so, and Pedro Vaquero's wife has parents given (Jose Munoz and Maria Sellanes) that Servando's act does not.] KEPT APART, NOT EXCLUDED. The first is the mother of Servando Tomas Gonzalez, named on his civil act of 1892 with her husband Juan Nepomuceno Gonzalez; the second is the mother of Manuel Vaquero, named on his civil act of 1889 and the San Jose baptism of 1864 with her husband Pedro Vaquero. Muniz is a common name; no act joins them.

Gualberto LERENA (m. Rosaura Alvarez) || Juan Ramon LERENA Fernandez (chr.1862)   # Probably one man, not merged: Juan Gualberto Lerena, twenty-three at his marriage on 25 June 1885, is a son of Alejandro Florencio and Severa Fernandez; Juan Ramon, born 12 July 1862, is a son of the same couple, and of the sons held he is the one whose birth falls within a year of 1862. The names Gualberto and Ramon differ, and the baptism of Juan Ramon is a machine transcript with a garbled second name; the plate of it would settle.
Juan Francisco CARDOSO || Juan Francisco ALVAREZ (father of Rosaura Alvarez)   # Different men: the first is a priest of Canelones in 1791; the second is the father of a bride of 1885.
Juan Francisco ALVAREZ Fernandez (b. 1829, San Jose) || Juan Francisco ALVAREZ (father of Rosaura Alvarez)   # POSSIBLY ONE MAN, NOT MERGED: the dispensation of the third degree by the equal lateral line between Juan Gualberto Lerena and Rosaura Alvarez (1885) needs a common great-grandparent, and Juan Fernandez with Barbara Sotura fit twice - Juan Francisco Alvarez, born 1829 to Josefa Fernandez, is their grandson, and Gualberto's mother Severa Fernandez is a daughter of their son Manuel; but Clara Pelaez, if she is a daughter of Francisco Pelaez and Maria Fernandez Alvarez, would give the same link from the mother's side. Nothing names the father's parents. Test: a baptism or death act of Rosaura's father or of Clara Pelaez naming their parents.

Gabino L. LLERENA || Gabino LERENA (of Chamanga, d. 1890)   # POSSIBLY ONE MAN, NOT MERGED: the first is an indexed marriage of a Gabino L. Llerena of Rosario, Colonia, with a Ramon Llerena beside him (no date or bride read); the second died at Chamanga on 9 February 1890 aged about eighty-three, a son of Ramon Lerena and Maria Gonzalez, married to Anastasia Moyano. The father's name and the surname agree; nothing else is compared.
Pedro Francisco FERNANDEZ Lerena (b. 1828, San Jose) || Francisco FERNANDEZ (m. Fulgencia Romero)   # POSSIBLY ONE MAN, NOT MERGED: the first was born at San Jose on 29 April 1828, a son of Francisco Fernandez Alvarez and Clara Lerena; the second is a Francisco Fernandez of Trinidad, godfather and father of a child in 1858, wife Fulgencia Romero. A civil act of 19 March 1894 on a Francisco Fernandez of sixty-five, a son of Francisco Fernandez and Clara Lerena, fits the first exactly by age; nothing names his wife or links the two rows.
